========== apps\mini-app\src\lib\api.ts ==========

export const API_URL = '';

export interface ApiResponse<T = any> {
  ok: boolean;
  data?: T;
  error?: string;
  status?: number;
}

class ApiClient {
  private baseUrl: string;

  constructor(baseUrl: string) {
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl;
  }

  private getToken(): string | null {
    return localStorage.getItem('token');
  }

  async request<T = any>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<ApiResponse<T>> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}${path}`;
    const token = this.getToken();

    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...((options.headers as Record<string, string>) || {}),
    };

    if (token) {
      headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(url, { ...options, headers });

      let data: any = null;
      const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type');
      if (contentType?.includes('application/json')) {
        data = await res.json();
      }

      if (!res.ok) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          error: data?.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`,
          status: res.status,
        };
      }

      return { ok: true, data, status: res.status };
    } catch (e: any) {
      return { ok: false, error: e.message || 'network_error' };
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Auth
  // ============================================================
  async authTelegram(initData: string): Promise<ApiResponse<{ token: string; user: any }>> {
    return this.request('/api/auth/telegram', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ initData }),
    });
  }

  async me(): Promise<ApiResponse<{ user: any; wallet: any; free_chips: any }>> {
    return this.request('/api/me');
  }

  async updateAvatar(avatarId: string): Promise<ApiResponse<{ ok: boolean; avatar_id: string }>> {
    return this.request('/api/me/avatar', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ avatar_id: avatarId }),
    });
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Tables
  // ============================================================
  async tables(): Promise<ApiResponse<{ tables: any[] }>> {
    return this.request('/api/tables');
  }

  async getTables(): Promise<ApiResponse<{ tables: any[] }>> {
    return this.request('/api/tables');
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Leaderboard / Events
  // ============================================================
  async leaderboard(period: string = 'all'): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request(`/api/leaderboard?period=${period}`);
  }

  async getLeaderboard(period: string = 'all'): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request(`/api/leaderboard?period=${period}`);
  }

  async getSeasonLeaderboard(): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/leaderboard/season');
  }

  async getMySeasonRank(): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/leaderboard/season/me');
  }

  async getMyTournaments(filter: string = 'all', limit: number = 50): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request(`/api/my/tournaments?filter=${filter}&limit=${limit}`);
  }

  async events(): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/events');
  }

  async getEvents(): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/events');
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Hands
  // ============================================================
  async getRecentHands(limit = 20, offset = 0): Promise<ApiResponse<{ hands: any[] }>> {
    return this.request(`/api/hands/recent?limit=${limit}&offset=${offset}`);
  }

  async getHandDetail(handId: number): Promise<ApiResponse<{ hand: any; events: any[] }>> {
    return this.request(`/api/hands/${handId}`);
  }

  async getHandReplay(
    handId: number,
  ): Promise<ApiResponse<{ hand: any; initialSnapshot: any; events: any[] }>> {
    return this.request(`/api/hands/${handId}/replay`);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Wallet
  // ============================================================
  async walletBalance(): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/wallet/balance');
  }

  async walletDepositInfo(): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/wallet/deposit-info');
  }

  async walletHistory(): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/wallet/history');
  }

  async walletWithdraw(amountTon: number, toAddress: string): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request('/api/wallet/withdraw', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ amountTon, toAddress }),
    });
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Tournaments
  // ============================================================
  async listTournaments(filter?: {
    status?: string;
    variant?: string;
    mode?: string;
  }): Promise<ApiResponse<{ tournaments: any[] }>> {
    const qs = new URLSearchParams();
    if (filter?.status) qs.set('status', filter.status);
    if (filter?.variant) qs.set('variant', filter.variant);
    if (filter?.mode) qs.set('mode', filter.mode);
    const q = qs.toString();
    return this.request(`/api/tournaments${q ? '?' + q : ''}`);
  }

  async getTournament(id: number): Promise<ApiResponse<any>> {
    return this.request(`/api/tournaments/${id}`);
  }

  async createTournament(body: any): Promise<ApiResponse<{ ok: boolean; tournamentId: number }>> {
    return this.request('/api/tournaments', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  }

  async registerTournament(id: number): Promise<ApiResponse<{ ok: boolean }>> {
    return this.request(`/api/tournaments/${id}/register`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    });
  }

  async unregisterTournament(id: number): Promise<ApiResponse<{ ok: boolean }>> {
    return this.request(`/api/tournaments/${id}/unregister`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    });
  }

  async startTournament(id: number): Promise<ApiResponse<{ ok: boolean }>> {
    return this.request(`/api/tournaments/${id}/start`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    });
  }

  async listBlindStructures(): Promise<ApiResponse<{ structures: any[] }>> {
    return this.request('/api/blind-structures');
  }
}

export const api = new ApiClient(API_URL);

========== apps\mini-app\src\lib\notifications.ts ==========

import { writable } from 'svelte/store';

export interface Notif {
  id: string;
  icon: string;
  title: string;
  message?: string;
  color: string;
  ts: number;
}

export const notifications = writable<Notif[]>([]);

export function pushNotif(n: Omit<Notif, 'id' | 'ts'>): void {
  const id = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
  const item: Notif = { ...n, id, ts: Date.now() };
  notifications.update((arr) => {
    // Cap at 4 toasts
    const next = [...arr, item];
    return next.length > 4 ? next.slice(next.length - 4) : next;
  });
  setTimeout(() => {
    notifications.update((arr) => arr.filter((x) => x.id !== id));
  }, 5000);
}

export function dismissNotif(id: string): void {
  notifications.update((arr) => arr.filter((x) => x.id !== id));
}


========== apps\mini-app\src\lib\realtime.ts ==========

import { io, type Socket } from 'socket.io-client';
import { writable } from 'svelte/store';

const API_URL = '';

let socket: Socket | null = null;

export const realtimeConnected = writable(false);

export function initRealtime(token: string): Socket {
  if (socket) {
    // If already connected, ensure auth is up to date
    (socket.auth as any) = { token };
    if (!socket.connected) socket.connect();
    return socket;
  }

  socket = io(API_URL, {
    auth: { token },
    transports: ['websocket'],
    reconnection: true,
    reconnectionDelay: 1000,
    reconnectionDelayMax: 5000,
  });

  socket.on('connect', () => {
    realtimeConnected.set(true);
    console.log('[realtime] connected', socket?.id);
  });

  socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
    realtimeConnected.set(false);
    console.log('[realtime] disconnected:', reason);
  });

  socket.on('connect_error', (err) => {
    console.warn('[realtime] connect_error:', err.message);
  });

  return socket;
}

export function getRealtime(): Socket | null {
  return socket;
}

export function closeRealtime(): void {
  if (socket) {
    socket.disconnect();
    socket = null;
    realtimeConnected.set(false);
  }
}

// Regex to match tournament events:
// tournament:123:level, tournament:123:elimination, etc.
export const TOURNAMENT_EVENT_RE =
  /^tournament:(\d+):(level|elimination|balance|final_table|finished|started|paused|resumed|break_start|break_end|late_reg_closed)$/;


========== apps\mini-app\src\lib\socket.ts ==========

import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client';

const API_URL = '';

export interface TableState {
  seats: any[];
  community: any[];
  phase: string;
  currentSeat: number;
  pot: number;
  minRaise: number;
  smallBlind: number;
  bigBlind: number;
  dealerSeat: number;
  winners: number[] | null;
  handNo: number;
  sequence: number;
  serverSeedHash: string;
  serverSeed?: string;
  serverTime: number;
  turnStartedAt: number;
  autoActionAt: number;
  turnBaseLimit: number;
  timeBankLimit: number;
  turnBankActive?: boolean;
  variant?: string;
  mode?: 'free' | 'real';
  totalRake?: number;
  totalRebuy?: number;
}

export type SocketEvent =
  | { type: 'state'; state: TableState }
  | { type: 'hand_result'; result: any; state: any }
  | { type: 'connected' }
  | { type: 'disconnected'; reason: string }
  | { type: 'reconnecting'; attempt: number }
  | { type: 'reconnected'; syncType: string }
  | { type: 'player_joined'; userId: number; displayName: string; total: number }
  | { type: 'player_left'; userId: number; total: number }
  | { type: 'error'; message: string; code?: string };

type Listener = (e: SocketEvent) => void;

export class TableSocketIO {
  private socket: Socket | null = null;
  private listeners: Set<Listener> = new Set();
  private token: string;
  private tableId: number;
  private closedByUser = false;

  // 🔴 Track sequence for resync
  private lastHandNo: number = 0;
  private lastSequence: number = 0;
  private reconnectAttempt = 0;

  constructor(tableId: number, token: string) {
    this.tableId = tableId;
    this.token = token;
  }

  connect(): void {
    this.closedByUser = false;

    this.socket = io(API_URL, {
      auth: {
        token: this.token,
        device: this.getDeviceHash(),
      },
      transports: ['websocket', 'polling'],
      reconnection: true,
      reconnectionDelay: 1000,
      reconnectionDelayMax: 5000,
      reconnectionAttempts: Infinity,
      timeout: 20000,
    });

    this.socket.on('connect', () => {
      const isReconnect = this.reconnectAttempt > 0;
      this.emit({ type: 'connected' });
      this.reconnectAttempt = 0;

      this.socket?.emit(
        'table:join',
        {
          tableId: this.tableId,
          lastHandNo: this.lastHandNo,
          lastSequence: this.lastSequence,
        },
        (res: any) => {
          if (!res?.ok) {
            this.emit({ type: 'error', message: res?.error ?? 'join_failed', code: res?.code });
            return;
          }
          if (isReconnect) {
            this.emit({ type: 'reconnected', syncType: res.syncType || 'full' });
          }
        },
      );
    });

    this.socket.on('table:state', (state: TableState) => {
      // 🔴 Track sequence
      if (typeof state.handNo === 'number') this.lastHandNo = state.handNo;
      if (typeof state.sequence === 'number') this.lastSequence = state.sequence;

      this.emit({ type: 'state', state });
    });

    this.socket.on('table:hand_result', (data: any) => {
      if (data?.state?.handNo) this.lastHandNo = data.state.handNo;
      this.emit({
        type: 'hand_result',
        result: data?.result,
        state: data?.state,
      });
    });

    this.socket.on('table:player_joined', (data: any) => {
      this.emit({
        type: 'player_joined',
        userId: data.userId,
        displayName: data.displayName,
        total: data.total,
      });
    });

    this.socket.on('table:player_left', (data: any) => {
      this.emit({
        type: 'player_left',
        userId: data.userId,
        total: data.total,
      });
    });

    this.socket.on('connect_error', (err: any) => {
      this.emit({ type: 'error', message: err.message });
    });

    this.socket.io.on('reconnect_attempt', (attempt: number) => {
      this.reconnectAttempt = attempt;
      this.emit({ type: 'reconnecting', attempt });
    });

    this.socket.on('disconnect', (reason: string) => {
      if (!this.closedByUser) {
        this.emit({ type: 'disconnected', reason });
      }
    });
  }

  private getDeviceHash(): string {
    let device = localStorage.getItem('device_hash');
    if (!device) {
      device = 'dev_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15);
      localStorage.setItem('device_hash', device);
    }
    return device;
  }

  private emit(event: SocketEvent): void {
    for (const l of this.listeners) {
      try {
        l(event);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      }
    }
  }

  action(action: 'FOLD' | 'CHECK' | 'CALL' | 'RAISE', amount = 0): void {
    if (!this.socket?.connected) return;

    // 🔴 Generate unique commandId for idempotency
    const commandId = this.generateCommandId();

    this.socket.emit('table:action', { action, amount, commandId }, (res: any) => {
      if (!res?.ok) {
        this.emit({ type: 'error', message: res?.error ?? 'action_failed', code: res?.code });
      }
    });
  }

  private generateCommandId(): string {
    return `${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 12)}`;
  }

  useTimeBank(): void {
    if (!this.socket?.connected) return;
    this.socket.emit('table:time_bank', {}, () => {});
  }

  requestResync(): void {
    if (!this.socket?.connected) return;
    this.socket.emit(
      'table:resync',
      {
        tableId: this.tableId,
        lastHandNo: this.lastHandNo,
        lastSequence: this.lastSequence,
      },
      (res: any) => {
        if (res?.ok) {
          this.emit({ type: 'reconnected', syncType: res.syncType || 'full' });
        }
      },
    );
  }

  leaveTable(): void {
    if (!this.socket?.connected) return;
    this.socket.emit('table:leave', {}, () => {});
  }

  on(listener: Listener): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  }

  close(): void {
    this.closedByUser = true;
    this.listeners.clear();
    if (this.socket) {
      this.socket.disconnect();
      this.socket = null;
    }
  }
}

<script lang="ts">

    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3 relative">
      <div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-2xl font-extrabold text-near-black"
           style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15); box-shadow: 0 4px 12px rgba(212,175,55,0.5);">
        {level}
      </div>
      <div class="flex-1">
        <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-gold/80 font-bold">Level</p>
        <p class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white tabular-nums">{level}</p>
        <p class="text-[10px] text-white/50">{handsPlayed} total hands</p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="relative">
      <div class="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-white/60 mb-1">
        <span>XP Progress</span>
        <span class="tabular-nums">{xpInLevel} / 50</span>
      </div>
      <div class="w-full h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden" style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4);">
        <div class="h-full rounded-full transition-all"
             style="width: {xpProgress}%; background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ffe9a8, #f4c95d, #a87922); box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(244,201,93,0.7);"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- STATS -->
  <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-5 px-4">
    <div class="rounded-2xl p-4 text-center" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.2);">
      <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">Unlocked</p>
      <p class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-400 tabular-nums">{unlockedCount}</p>
      <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40">of {totalCount}</p>
    </div>
    <div class="rounded-2xl p-4 text-center" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.2);">
      <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">Progress</p>
      <p class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-gold tabular-nums">{Math.round((unlockedCount / totalCount) * 100)}%</p>
      <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40">complete</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- LIST -->
  <h2 class="text-base font-extrabold mb-3 px-4 text-white/95">All Achievements</h2>
  <div class="space-y-2.5 px-4">
    {#each achievements as a}
      {@const cur = a.current()}
      {@const done = cur >= a.target}
      {@const pct = Math.min(100, (cur / a.target) * 100)}
      {@const rc = rarityColors[a.rarity]}
      <div class="rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-center gap-3"
           style="background: {rc.bg}; border: 1px solid {done ? rc.border : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)'}; {done ? `box-shadow: 0 0 16px ${rc.glow}40;` : ''}">
        <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shrink-0 {!done ? 'grayscale opacity-40' : ''}"
             style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.35); border: 1px solid {rc.border};">
          {a.icon}
        </div>

        <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
            <p class="text-sm font-extrabold truncate {done ? 'text-white' : 'text-white/60'}">{a.title}</p>
            {#if done}
              <span class="text-[8px] font-extrabold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-near-black"
                    style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15);">✓ DONE</span>
            {/if}
          </div>
          <p class="text-[10px] text-white/50 truncate">{a.desc}</p>

          {#if !done}
            <div class="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-2">
              <div class="flex-1 h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden" style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4);">
                <div class="h-full rounded-full" style="width: {pct}%; background: {rc.border};"></div>
              </div>
              <span class="text-[9px] font-mono text-white/50 tabular-nums shrink-0">{cur}/{a.target}</span>
            </div>
          {/if}
        </div>

        <div class="text-right shrink-0">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase font-bold">Reward</p>
          <p class="text-[11px] font-extrabold {done ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-gold'}">{a.reward}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    {/each}
  </div>
</div>


========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Admin.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};

  const API = '';
  const STORAGE_KEY = 'admin_secret';

  let adminSecret = '';
  let authed = false;
  let loading = false;
  let error = '';
  let toast = '';

  let stats: any = null;
  let tournaments: any[] = [];
    let blindStructures: any[] = [];
  let templates: any[] = [];
  let showTemplateForm = false;
  let creatingTemplate = false;

  let tform = {
    name: '',
    variant: 'holdem',
    mode: 'free',
    format: 'sng',
    speed: 'regular',
    buyIn: 0,
    fee: 0,
    startingStack: 5000,
    minPlayers: 2,
    maxPlayers: 100,
    guaranteed: 0,
    lateRegLevel: 0,
    allowReEntry: false,
    maxReEntries: 1,
    isBounty: false,
    bountyAmount: 0,
    blindStructureId: 0,
    scheduleType: 'daily' as 'hourly' | 'daily' | 'weekly',
    scheduleTime: '14:00',
    scheduleDay: 0,
  };

  let showCreate = false;
  let creating = false;

  let form = {
    name: '',
    variant: 'holdem',
    mode: 'free',
    format: 'sng',
    speed: 'regular',
    buyIn: 0,
    fee: 0,
    startingStack: 1500,
    minPlayers: 2,
    maxPlayers: 9,
    guaranteed: 0,
    lateRegLevel: 0,
    allowReEntry: false,
    maxReEntries: 1,
    isBounty: false,
    bountyAmount: 0,
    satelliteTargetId: null as number | null,
    seatsAwarded: 0,
    blindStructureId: 0,
  };

  function headers() {
    return {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Authorization: `Bearer ${adminSecret}`,
    };
  }

  function flash(msg: string) {
    toast = msg;
    setTimeout(() => (toast = ''), 3500);
  }

  async function login() {
    if (!adminSecret) return;
    loading = true;
    error = '';
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/stats`, { headers: headers() });
      if (res.ok) {
        authed = true;
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, adminSecret);
        await loadAll();
      } else {
        error = 'Invalid admin secret';
        adminSecret = '';
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      error = e.message;
    } finally {
      loading = false;
    }
  }

  async function loadAll() {
    loading = true;
    try {
      const [s, t, b] = await Promise.all([
        fetch(`${API}/api/admin/stats`, { headers: headers() }).then((r) => r.json()),
        fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournaments`, { headers: headers() }).then((r) => r.json()),
        fetch(`${API}/api/blind-structures`).then((r) => r.json()),
      ]);
      stats = s;
            tournaments = t.tournaments || [];

      // Also load templates
      const tplRes = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournament-templates`, {
        headers: headers(),
      }).then((r) => r.json());
      templates = tplRes.templates || [];
      blindStructures = b.structures || [];
      if (blindStructures.length > 0 && !form.blindStructureId) {
        form.blindStructureId = blindStructures[0].id;
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      error = e.message;
    } finally {
      loading = false;
    }
  }

  async function createTournament() {
    if (!form.name.trim()) {
      error = 'Tournament name is required';
      return;
    }
    creating = true;
    error = '';
    try {
      const payload = {
        ...form,
        startingStack: Number(form.startingStack) || 1500,
        buyIn: Number(form.buyIn) || 0,
        fee: Number(form.fee) || 0,
        minPlayers: Number(form.minPlayers) || 2,
        maxPlayers: Number(form.maxPlayers) || 9,
        guaranteed: Number(form.guaranteed) || 0,
        lateRegLevel: Number(form.lateRegLevel) || 0,
        maxReEntries: Number(form.maxReEntries) || 1,
        bountyAmount: Number(form.bountyAmount) || 0,
        seatsAwarded: Number(form.seatsAwarded) || 0,
        blindStructureId: Number(form.blindStructureId),
      };
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/tournaments`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: headers(),
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        flash(`✅ Tournament #${data.tournamentId} created`);
        showCreate = false;
        form.name = '';
        await loadAll();
      } else if (res.status === 401) {
        error = 'Admin secret rejected';
      } else {
        error = data.error || 'create_failed';
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      error = e.message;
    } finally {
      creating = false;
    }
  }

  async function startTournament(id: number) {
    if (!confirm(`Start tournament #${id}?`)) return;
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/tournaments/${id}/start`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: headers(),
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      flash(`🚀 Tournament #${id} started`);
      await loadAll();
    } else {
      error = data.error || 'start_failed';
    }
  }

  async function cancelTournament(id: number) {
    if (!confirm(`Cancel tournament #${id} and refund all players?`)) return;
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournaments/${id}/cancel`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: headers(),
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      flash(`❌ Cancelled #${id}, refunded ${data.refunded} players`);
      await loadAll();
    } else {
      error = data.error || 'cancel_failed';
    }
  }

  async function pauseTournament(id: number) {
    if (!confirm(`Pause tournament #${id}?`)) return;
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournaments/${id}/pause`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: headers(),
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      flash(`⏸️ Tournament #${id} paused`);
      await loadAll();
    } else {
      error = data.error || 'pause_failed';
    }
  }

  async function resumeTournament(id: number) {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournaments/${id}/resume`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: headers(),
      body: JSON.stringify({}),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      flash(`▶️ Tournament #${id} resumed`);
      await loadAll();
    } else {
      error = data.error || 'resume_failed';
    }
  }
  async function createTemplate() {
    if (!tform.name.trim()) {
      error = 'Template name is required';
      return;
    }
    creatingTemplate = true;
    error = '';
    try {
      const payload = {
        ...tform,
        blindStructureId: Number(tform.blindStructureId),
      };
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournament-templates`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: headers(),
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        flash(`✅ Template #${data.templateId} created`);
        showTemplateForm = false;
        tform.name = '';
        await loadAll();
      } else {
        error = data.error || 'create_template_failed';
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      error = e.message;
    } finally {
      creatingTemplate = false;
    }
  }

  async function toggleTemplate(id: number, active: boolean) {
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournament-templates/${id}/toggle`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: headers(),
      body: JSON.stringify({ active }),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      flash(active ? '▶️ Activated' : '⏸️ Paused');
      await loadAll();
    } else {
      error = 'toggle_failed';
    }
  }

  async function deleteTemplate(id: number) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete template #${id}?`)) return;
    const res = await fetch(`${API}/api/admin/tournament-templates/${id}`, {
      method: 'DELETE',
      headers: headers(),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      flash('🗑️ Deleted');
      await loadAll();
    } else {
      error = 'delete_failed';
    }
  }

  function fmtDate(iso: string | null): string {
    if (!iso) return '—';
    try {
      return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-US', {
        month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
      });
    } catch { return '—'; }
  }

  function logout() {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    adminSecret = '';
    authed = false;
    stats = null;
    tournaments = [];
  }

  function statusColor(s: string): string {
    if (s === 'running') return '#10b981';
    if (s === 'final_table') return '#f4c95d';
    if (s === 'registering') return '#22d3ee';
    if (s === 'scheduled') return '#8b5cf6';
    if (s === 'finished') return '#6b7280';
    if (s === 'paused') return '#f59e0b';
    if (s === 'cancelled') return '#ef4444';
    return '#6b7280';
  }

  onMount(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (saved) {
      adminSecret = saved;
      login();
    }
  });
</script>

<div class="px-4 pb-28" style="min-height: 100dvh;">
  <TopBar />

  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-3 mb-4">
    <button
      on:click={onBack}
      aria-label="Back"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white/70 active:scale-95"
      style="background: rgba(139,92,246,0.15); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
    >
      ←
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold flex-1">🛠️ Admin Panel</h1>
    {#if authed}
      <button
        on:click={logout}
        class="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-300 px-2 py-1 rounded-full"
        style="background: rgba(220,38,38,0.15);"
      >
        Logout
      </button>
    {/if}
  </div>

  {#if !authed}
    <div class="card space-y-3 mt-8">
      <div class="text-center mb-2">
        <p class="text-4xl mb-2">🔒</p>
        <p class="text-sm text-white/60">Enter admin secret to continue</p>
      </div>
      <input
        type="password"
        bind:value={adminSecret}
        on:keydown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && login()}
        placeholder="Admin secret"
        class="w-full rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none"
        style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
      />
      <button
        on:click={login}
        disabled={loading || !adminSecret}
        class="w-full btn-gold disabled:opacity-40"
      >
        {loading ? 'Checking…' : 'Login'}
      </button>
      {#if error}
        <p class="text-xs text-red-300 text-center">{error}</p>
      {/if}
    </div>
  {:else}
    {#if stats}
      <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-4">
        <div class="card !p-3 text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Total</p>
          <p class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white tabular-nums">{stats.totalTournaments}</p>
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40">tournaments</p>
        </div>
        <div class="card !p-3 text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Running</p>
          <p class="text-xl font-extrabold text-emerald-400 tabular-nums">
            {stats.byStatus?.running || 0}
          </p>
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40">now</p>
        </div>
        <div class="card !p-3 text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Players</p>
          <p class="text-xl font-extrabold text-cyan tabular-nums">
            {stats.totalUniquePlayers}
          </p>
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40">unique</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-4">
        <div class="card !p-3 text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase font-bold">Rake</p>
          <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-gold tabular-nums">
            ${Number(stats.totalRake || 0).toFixed(2)}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div class="card !p-3 text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase font-bold">By Status</p>
          <div class="text-[10px] text-white/70 font-mono mt-1">
            {#each Object.entries(stats.byStatus || {}) as [k, v]}
              <span class="mr-1.5">{k}:{v}</span>
            {/each}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    {/if}

    <div class="flex gap-2 mb-3">
      <button
        on:click={() => (showCreate = !showCreate)}
        class="flex-1 btn-gold flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
      >
        {showCreate ? '✕ Close' : '+ Create Tournament'}
      </button>
      <button
        on:click={loadAll}
        aria-label="Refresh"
        class="w-12 rounded-xl text-white/70 active:scale-95"
        style="background: rgba(139,92,246,0.15); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.3);"
      >
        🔄
      </button>
    </div>

    {#if showCreate}
      <div class="card space-y-3 mb-4">
        <div>
          <label for="t-name" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
            Tournament Name
          </label>
          <input
            id="t-name"
            type="text"
            bind:value={form.name}
            placeholder="e.g. Daily Deepstack"
            class="w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-white outline-none"
            style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
          />
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <div>
            <label for="t-variant" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Game Type
            </label>
            <select
              id="t-variant"
              bind:value={form.variant}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            >
              <option value="holdem">Hold'em</option>
              <option value="omaha4">Omaha 4</option>
              <option value="omaha5">Omaha 5</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label for="t-mode" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Mode
            </label>
            <select
              id="t-mode"
              bind:value={form.mode}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            >
              <option value="free">Free (Play Money)</option>
              <option value="real">Real (USD)</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label for="t-format" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Format
            </label>
            <select
              id="t-format"
              bind:value={form.format}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            >
              <option value="sng">SNG</option>
              <option value="mtt">MTT</option>
              <option value="freeroll">Freeroll</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <div>
            <label for="t-buyin" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Buy-in ($)
            </label>
            <input
              id="t-buyin"
              type="number"
              min="0"
              step="0.01"
              bind:value={form.buyIn}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label for="t-fee" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Fee ($)
            </label>
            <input
              id="t-fee"
              type="number"
              min="0"
              step="0.01"
              bind:value={form.fee}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label for="t-stack" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Starting Stack
            </label>
            <input
              id="t-stack"
              type="number"
              min="100"
              step="100"
              bind:value={form.startingStack}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <div>
            <label for="t-min" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Min Players
            </label>
            <input
              id="t-min"
              type="number"
              min="2"
              bind:value={form.minPlayers}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label for="t-max" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Max Players
            </label>
            <input
              id="t-max"
              type="number"
              min="2"
              bind:value={form.maxPlayers}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label for="t-speed" class="block text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/50 font-bold mb-1">
              Speed
            </label>
            <select
              id="t-speed"
              bind:value={form.speed}
              class="w-full rounded-lg px-2 py-2.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            >
              <option value="regular">Regular</option>
              <option value="turbo">Turbo</option>

              Late Reg Until Level
            </label>
            <input
              id="t-late-level"
              type="number"
              min="0"
              bind:value={form.lateRegLevel}

              style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.25);"
            >
              {#if blindStructures.length === 0}
                <option value={0}>Loading…</option>

                    {b.name} · start {b.startingStack}

                id="t-reentry"
                type="checkbox"
                bind:checked={form.allowReEntry}
                class="w-4 h-4 accent-gold"
              />
              <span>Allow Re-entry</span>
            </label>
            {#if form.allowReEntry}
              <input
                type="number"
                min="1"
                bind:value={form.maxReEntries}

        <!-- ============================================================ -->
    <!-- D6.1: RECURRING TEMPLATES -->

    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-6 mb-3">
      <h2 class="text-base font-extrabold px-1 text-white/90">

    {#if showTemplateForm}
      <div class="card space-y-3 mb-3">

            Template Name

            type="text"

        <p class="text-2xl mb-1">🗓️</p>
        <p class="text-white/50 text-xs">No recurring templates yet</p>

                    style="background: {tpl.isActive ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.25)' : 'rgba(107,114,128,0.25)'}; color: {tpl.isActive ? '#6ee7b7' : '#9ca3af'}; border: 1px solid {tpl.isActive ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.5)' : 'rgba(107,114,128,0.5)'};"
                  >
                    {tpl.isActive ? 'ACTIVE' : 'PAUSED'}
                  </span>
                  <span class="text-[9px] text-cyan/80 font-mono">
                    {tpl.scheduleType}
                    {tpl.scheduleType !== 'hourly' ? ` @ ${tpl.scheduleTime}` : ''}
                    {tpl.scheduleType === 'weekly' ? ` (${['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat'][tpl.scheduleDay]})` : ''}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p class="text-sm font-extrabold text-white truncate">{tpl.name}</p>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40">
                  {tpl.variant.toUpperCase()} · {tpl.format} · {tpl.buyIn > 0 ? `$${tpl.buyIn}` : 'FREE'}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div class="text-right shrink-0 ml-2">
                <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40">Next run</p>
                <p class="text-[10px] font-mono text-gold">{fmtDate(tpl.nextRunAt)}</p>
                {#if tpl.lastRunAt}
                  <p class="text-[9px] text-white/30 mt-0.5">
                    Last: {fmtDate(tpl.lastRunAt)}
                  </p>
                {/if}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex gap-2">
              <button
                on:click={() => toggleTemplate(tpl.id, !tpl.isActive)}
                class="flex-1 rounded-lg py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold active:scale-95"
                style="background: {tpl.isActive ? 'rgba(245,158,11,0.15)' : 'linear-gradient(180deg, #22c55e, #15803d)'}; color: {tpl.isActive ? '#fcd34d' : 'white'}; border: 1px solid {tpl.isActive ? 'rgba(245,158,11,0.35)' : 'transparent'};"
              >
                {tpl.isActive ? '⏸️ Pause' : '▶️ Activate'}
              </button>
              <button
                on:click={() => deleteTemplate(tpl.id)}
                class="rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-red-300 active:scale-95"
                style="background: rgba(220,38,38,0.15); border: 1px solid rgba(220,38,38,0.35);"
              >
                🗑️
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        {/each}
      </div>
    {/if}

  {/if}

  {#if toast}
    <div
      class="fixed top-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 rounded-2xl px-4 py-2 max-w-[90%]"
      style="background: rgba(6,78,59,0.95); border: 1px solid rgba(34,197,94,0.5);"
    >
      <p class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-100">{toast}</p>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>


========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Events.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount, onDestroy } from 'svelte';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';
  import Avatar from '../components/Avatar.svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import { auth } from '../stores/auth';

  export let onNavigate: (page: any) => void = () => {};

  let events: any[] = [];
  let loading = true;
  let selectedEvent: any = null;
  let eventDetail: any = null;
  let loadingDetail = false;
  let actionMsg = '';
  let actionMsgType: 'success' | 'error' = 'success';
  let processing = false;
  let refreshTimer: any = null;

  function fmt(n: any): string {
    return Number(Number(n || 0).toFixed(2)).toFixed(2);
  }

  function fmtDate(d: string): string {
    try {
      return new Date(d).toLocaleString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    } catch { return d; }
  }

  function timeUntil(d: string): string {
    const diff = new Date(d).getTime() - Date.now();
    if (diff <= 0) return 'Now';
    const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
    if (mins < 60) return `in ${mins}m`;
    const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
    if (hrs < 24) return `in ${hrs}h`;
    return `in ${Math.floor(hrs / 24)}d`;
  }

  function statusBadge(status: string): { text: string; color: string } {
    switch (status) {
      case 'scheduled':   return { text: 'UPCOMING', color: '#8b5cf6' };
      case 'registering': return { text: 'REGISTERING', color: '#22d3ee' };
      case 'running':     return { text: '● LIVE', color: '#10b981' };
      case 'finished':    return { text: 'FINISHED', color: '#6b7280' };
      case 'cancelled':   return { text: 'CANCELLED', color: '#ef4444' };
      default:            return { text: status.toUpperCase(), color: '#6b7280' };
    }
  }

  function isRegistered(ev: any): boolean {
    if (!ev) return false;
    const myId = $auth.user?.id;
    if (!myId) return false;
    return ev.registrations?.some((r: any) => r.user_id === myId);
  }

  function myRegistration(ev: any): any {
    if (!ev) return null;
    const myId = $auth.user?.id;
    return ev.registrations?.find((r: any) => r.user_id === myId);
  }

  async function loadEvents() {
    const res = await api.events();
    if (res.ok && res.data) events = res.data.events;
    loading = false;
  }

  async function openEvent(ev: any) {
    selectedEvent = ev;
    loadingDetail = true;
    eventDetail = null;
    const res = await api.event(ev.id);
    if (res.ok && res.data) eventDetail = res.data;
    loadingDetail = false;
  }

  function closeModal() {
    selectedEvent = null;
    eventDetail = null;
  }

  async function register() {
    if (!selectedEvent || processing) return;
    processing = true;
    actionMsg = '';
    const res = await api.registerEvent(selectedEvent.id);
    processing = false;
    if (res.ok) {
      actionMsg = res.data?.already ? 'Already registered' : '✓ Registered!';
      actionMsgType = 'success';
      await loadEvents();
      await openEvent(selectedEvent);
      setTimeout(() => (actionMsg = ''), 3000);
    } else {
      const errMap: Record<string, string> = {
        event_full: 'Tournament is full',
        insufficient_balance: 'Insufficient balance',
        event_not_open: 'Registration is closed',
      };
      actionMsg = '❌ ' + (errMap[res.error || ''] || 'Failed to register');
      actionMsgType = 'error';
      setTimeout(() => (actionMsg = ''), 4000);
    }
  }

  async function unregister() {
    if (!selectedEvent || processing) return;
    if (!confirm('Unregister? Buy-in will be refunded.')) return;
    processing = true;
    actionMsg = '';
    const res = await api.unregisterEvent(selectedEvent.id);
    processing = false;
    if (res.ok) {
      actionMsg = '✓ Unregistered (refunded)';
      actionMsgType = 'success';
      await loadEvents();
      await openEvent(selectedEvent);
      setTimeout(() => (actionMsg = ''), 3000);
    } else {
      actionMsg = '❌ Failed to unregister';
      actionMsgType = 'error';
      setTimeout(() => (actionMsg = ''), 4000);
    }
  }

  onMount(async () => {
    await loadEvents();
    refreshTimer = setInterval(loadEvents, 10000);
  });

  onDestroy(() => {
    if (refreshTimer) clearInterval(refreshTimer);
  });
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <TopBar />

  <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-3 mb-5 px-4">
    <h1 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white">Events</h1>
    <span class="text-[10px] rounded-full px-3 py-1 font-bold text-gold"

              </p>

              <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase font-bold">Players</p>

              </p>

            {/if}
          </div>
        </button>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>

  $: filtered = search.trim()
    ? faqs.map((cat) => ({
        ...cat,
        items: cat.items.filter(
          (i) => i.q.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || i.a.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()),

    expanded = expanded === id ? null : id;

</script>

                class="w-full text-left px-4 py-3 active:bg-white/5 transition"
                style="border-bottom: {idx < cat.items.length - 1 ? '1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.06)' : 'none'};"
              >
                <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <p class="text-[12px] font-bold text-white/90 flex-1">{item.q}</p>
                  <span
                    class="text-gold text-[14px] leading-none shrink-0 mt-0.5 transition-transform"
                    style="transform: rotate({expanded === `${cat.id}-${idx}` ? '45deg' : '0deg'});"
                  >

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Home.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import { auth } from '../stores/auth';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';
  import PlayingCard from '../components/PlayingCard.svelte';
  import PokerChip from '../components/PokerChip.svelte';
  import QuickSeatSheet from '../components/QuickSeatSheet.svelte';

  export let onNavigate: (page: any) => void = () => {};
  export let onQuickSeat: (variant: string, mode: 'free' | 'real') => void = () => {};
  export let onOpenTable: (id: number) => void = () => {};

  let sheetOpen = false;
  let liveTournaments: any[] = [];
  let tables: any[] = [];

  $: chips = Number($auth.freeChips?.chips || 0);

  const games = [
    { id: 'holdem', title: "Texas Hold'em", sub: 'No-Limit', emoji: '♠️', cards: [{ rank: 'A', suit: '♠' as const, rot: -12 }, { rank: 'K', suit: '♥' as const, rot: 8 }] },
    { id: 'omaha4', title: 'Omaha', sub: '4 Hole Cards', emoji: '♦️', cards: [{ rank: 'A', suit: '♦' as const, rot: -14 }, { rank: 'K', suit: '♠' as const, rot: 6 }] },
    { id: 'omaha5', title: '7-Card Stud', sub: 'Classic', emoji: '♣️', cards: [{ rank: 'A', suit: '♣' as const, rot: -10 }, { rank: 'K', suit: '♥' as const, rot: 10 }] },
    { id: 'tourney', title: 'Short Deck', sub: '6+ Holdem', emoji: '🎴', cards: [{ rank: 'A', suit: '♥' as const, rot: -8 }, { rank: 'K', suit: '♦' as const, rot: 12 }] },
  ];

  const features = [
    { icon: '⚡', title: 'Fast Payouts', sub: 'Get your winnings quickly' },
    { icon: '🌍', title: 'Global Players', sub: 'Play worldwide' },
    { icon: '🛡️', title: 'Secure & Fair', sub: 'Trusted platform' },
  ];

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const tablesRes = await api.tables();
      if (tablesRes.ok && tablesRes.data) tables = tablesRes.data.tables || [];
      const tourneyRes = await api.listTournaments({ status: 'running' });
      if (tourneyRes.ok && tourneyRes.data) liveTournaments = (tourneyRes.data.tournaments || []).slice(0, 3);
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  onMount(() => { loadData(); });

  function openGame(g: typeof games[0]) {
    if (g.id === 'tourney') { onNavigate('tournaments'); return; }
    onQuickSeat(g.id, 'free');
  }

  function handleConfirm(data: { variant: string; mode: 'free' | 'real' }) {
    sheetOpen = false;
    onQuickSeat(data.variant, data.mode);
  }
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <!-- TOP BAR -->
  <TopBar onOpenSettings={() => onNavigate('profile')} onOpenWallet={() => onNavigate('wallet')} />

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Leaderboard.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount, onDestroy } from 'svelte';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';
  import Avatar from '../components/Avatar.svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import { auth } from '../stores/auth';

  let loading = true;
  let season: any = null;
  let entries: any[] = [];
  let me: any = null;
  let refreshTimer: any = null;

  function fmt(n: any): string {
    return Number(Number(n || 0).toFixed(2)).toFixed(2);
  }

  function daysLeft(endAt: string | null): number | null {
    if (!endAt) return null;
    try {
      const diff = new Date(endAt).getTime() - Date.now();
      return Math.max(0, Math.ceil(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)));
    } catch { return null; }
  }

  async function loadAll() {
    try {
      const [lb, my] = await Promise.all([
        api.getSeasonLeaderboard(),
        api.getMySeasonRank(),
      ]);
      if (lb.ok && lb.data) {
        season = lb.data.season;
        entries = lb.data.entries || [];
      }
      if (my.ok && my.data) me = my.data.me;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[leaderboard]', e);
    } finally {
      loading = false;
    }
  }

  onMount(async () => {
    await loadAll();
    refreshTimer = setInterval(loadAll, 15000);
  });

  onDestroy(() => { if (refreshTimer) clearInterval(refreshTimer); });

  $: left = season ? daysLeft(season.endAt) : null;
  $: top3 = entries.slice(0, 3);
  $: rest = entries.slice(3);
  $: myId = $auth.user?.id;
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <TopBar />

  <!-- HEADER -->
  <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-3 mb-4 px-4">
    <div>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white">🏆 Leaderboard</h1>
      {#if season}
        <p class="text-[11px] text-white/50 mt-0.5">{season.name}</p>
      {/if}
    </div>
    {#if left !== null}
      <span
        class="text-[10px] rounded-full px-3 py-1 font-bold text-gold/80 font-mono"
        style="background: rgba(212,175,55,0.15); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.3);"
      >
        ⏳ {left}d left
      </span>
    {/if}
  </div>

        background:

      <p class="text-white/50 text-sm">No points yet this season</p>

  {:else}

    <!-- TOP 3 PODIUM -->

<script lang="ts">

      console.error(e);

    busy = false;
  }
</script>

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\MyTournaments.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';

  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};

  type Filter = 'all' | 'won' | 'itm' | 'eliminated';

  let filter: Filter = 'all';
  let loading = true;
  let tournaments: any[] = [];
  let stats: any = null;

  function fmt(n: any): string {
    return Number(Number(n || 0).toFixed(2)).toFixed(2);
  }

  function fmtDate(iso: string | null): string {
    if (!iso) return '—';
    try {
      return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-US', {
        month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
      });
    } catch { return '—'; }
  }

  function statusColor(t: any): string {
    if (t.isWinner) return '#f4c95d';
    if (t.payout > 0) return '#10b981';
    if (t.entryStatus === 'qualified') return '#a78bfa';
    return '#6b7280';
  }

  function statusLabel(t: any): string {
    if (t.isWinner) return '🏆 WON';
    if (t.entryStatus === 'qualified') return '🎟️ QUALIFIED';
    if (t.payout > 0) return '💰 ITM';
    if (t.entryStatus === 'eliminated') return '💀 OUT';
    if (t.entryStatus === 'refunded') return '↩️ REFUNDED';
    return t.entryStatus?.toUpperCase() || '—';
  }

  async function load() {
    loading = true;
    try {
      const res = await api.getMyTournaments(filter, 100);
      if (res.ok && res.data) {
        tournaments = res.data.tournaments || [];
        stats = res.data.stats;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    } finally {
      loading = false;
    }
  }

  function setFilter(f: Filter) {
    filter = f;
    load();
  }

  onMount(load);

  $: roiColor = stats ? (stats.roi >= 0 ? '#6ee7b7' : '#fca5a5') : '#fff';
</script>

<div class="px-4 pb-28" style="min-height: 100dvh;">
  <TopBar />

  <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 mb-4">
    <button
      on:click={onBack}
      aria-label="Back"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition"
      style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.7)); border: 1.5px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);"
    >
      <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
           stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white flex-1">My Tournaments</h1>
  </div>

  {#if stats}
    <!-- Summary stats -->
    <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-3">
      <div class="rounded-2xl p-3 text-center" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.2);">
        <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">Played</p>
        <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-white tabular-nums leading-none">{stats.total}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="rounded-2xl p-3 text-center" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.2);">
        <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">Wins</p>
        <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-gold tabular-nums leading-none">{stats.wins}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="rounded-2xl p-3 text-center" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.2);">
        <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">Points</p>

      </div>
    </div>

    <div
      class="rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4 relative overflow-hidden"
      style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(16,185,129,0.15), rgba(5,150,105,0.05)); border: 1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.3);"
    >
      <div class="absolute -top-8 -right-8 w-32 h-32 rounded-full opacity-20" style="background: radial-gradient(circle, #10b981, transparent 70%);"></div>

      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 relative">
        <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-300/80 font-bold">Net Profit</p>
        <p class="text-[10px] font-bold" style="color: {roiColor};">
          ROI {stats.roi >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{stats.roi}%
        </p>
      </div>
      <p class="text-3xl font-extrabold tabular-nums leading-tight relative" style="color: {stats.netProfit >= 0 ? '#6ee7b7' : '#fca5a5'};">
        {stats.netProfit >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${fmt(stats.netProfit)}
      </p>
      <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-3 pt-3 relative" style="border-top: 1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.2);">
        <div>
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase font-bold">Won</p>
          <p class="text-[12px] font-extrabold text-emerald-400 tabular-nums">${fmt(stats.totalWon)}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase font-bold">Invested</p>
          <p class="text-[12px] font-extrabold text-white/70 tabular-nums">${fmt(stats.totalInvested)}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase font-bold">ITM</p>
          <p class="text-[12px] font-extrabold text-cyan tabular-nums">{stats.itm} ({stats.itmPct}%)</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  {/if}

  <!-- Filters -->
  <div class="flex gap-1.5 mb-4 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1">
    {#each [
      { key: 'all' as Filter, label: 'All', color: '#d4af37' },
      { key: 'won' as Filter, label: '🏆 Wins', color: '#f4c95d' },
      { key: 'itm' as Filter, label: '💰 ITM', color: '#10b981' },
      { key: 'eliminated' as Filter, label: '💀 Out', color: '#6b7280' },
    ] as f}
      <button
        on:click={() => setFilter(f.key)}
        class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap active:scale-95 transition-all uppercase tracking-wider"
        style="background: {filter === f.key ? `linear-gradient(180deg, ${f.color}, ${f.color}bb)` : 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9))'}; color: {filter === f.key ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)'}; border: 1px solid {filter === f.key ? `${f.color}dd` : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.15)'};"
      >
        {f.label}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>

  {#if loading}
    <div class="text-center py-12 text-white/40 text-sm">Loading…</div>
  {:else if tournaments.length === 0}
    <div class="rounded-3xl p-8 text-center" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.6), rgba(11,19,36,0.85)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
      <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl mx-auto mb-3 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.15), rgba(168,122,21,0.08)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.3);">
        🎫
      </div>
      <p class="text-white/60 text-sm font-bold">No tournaments yet</p>
      <p class="text-white/40 text-[11px] mt-1">Play a tournament to see it here</p>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="space-y-2">
      {#each tournaments as t}
        {@const sc = statusColor(t)}
        <div
          class="rounded-2xl p-3 relative overflow-hidden"

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Notifications.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};

  interface Notification {
    id: string;
    type: 'tournament' | 'friend' | 'reward' | 'security' | 'system';
    title: string;
    message: string;
    time: string;
    read: boolean;
  }

  let notifications: Notification[] = [
    { id: '1', type: 'reward', title: 'Welcome Bonus', message: 'You received 1,000 free chips. Good luck!', time: '2 minutes ago', read: false },
    { id: '2', type: 'system', title: 'New Tournaments Available', message: 'Daily Deepstack is now open for registration.', time: '1 hour ago', read: false },
    { id: '3', type: 'security', title: 'New Login Detected', message: 'Your account was accessed from a new device.', time: '3 hours ago', read: true },
  ];

  const typeIcons: Record<string, string> = {
    tournament: '🏆',
    friend: '👥',
    reward: '🎁',
    security: '🔒',
    system: '📢',
  };

  const typeColors: Record<string, string> = {
    tournament: '#8b5cf6',
    friend: '#22d3ee',
    reward: '#f4c95d',
    security: '#ef4444',
    system: '#10b981',
  };

  function markAllRead() {
    notifications = notifications.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true }));
  }

  $: unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length;
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <TopBar />

  <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 mb-4 px-4">
    <button
      on:click={onBack}
      aria-label="Back"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition"
      style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.7)); border: 1.5px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);"
    >
      <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white flex-1">🔔 Notifications</h1>
    {#if unreadCount > 0}
      <button
        on:click={markAllRead}
        class="text-[10px] font-bold text-gold active:opacity-70 px-2 py-1 rounded-full"
        style="background: rgba(244,201,93,0.15);"
      >
        Mark all read
      </button>
    {/if}
  </div>

  {#if notifications.length === 0}
    <div class="rounded-2xl p-8 text-center mx-4" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
      <p class="text-4xl mb-2">🔔</p>
      <p class="text-white/50 text-sm">No notifications</p>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="space-y-2 px-4">
      {#each notifications as n}
        {@const color = typeColors[n.type]}
        <div
          class="rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-start gap-3 relative"
          style="
            background: {n.read
              ? 'rgba(16,26,58,0.5)'
              : 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(139,92,246,0.15), rgba(8,13,36,0.9))'};
            border: 1px solid {n.read ? 'rgba(139,92,246,0.15)' : 'rgba(139,92,246,0.35)'};
            box-shadow: {n.read ? 'none' : `0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.4)`};
            opacity: {n.read ? 0.75 : 1};
          "
        >
          <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-xl shrink-0"
               style="background: {color}25; border: 1px solid {color}50;">
            {typeIcons[n.type]}
          </div>

          <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
              <p class="text-sm font-extrabold text-white truncate">{n.title}</p>
              {#if !n.read}
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0" style="background: {color}; box-shadow: 0 0 6px {color};"></span>
              {/if}
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-white/60 leading-relaxed">{n.message}</p>
            <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 mt-1.5">{n.time}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>


========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Profile.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">

  import AvatarFramePicker from '../components/AvatarFramePicker.svelte';
  import { AVATARS } from '../lib/avatars';

  export let onNavigate: (page: any) => void = () => {};

  $: user = $auth.user;
  $: chips = $auth.freeChips;
  $: selectedAvatar = (user as any)?.avatar_id || 'king';
  $: selectedFrame = (user as any)?.frame_id || 'royal';

  $: handsPlayed = chips?.total_hands || 0;
  $: level = Math.floor(handsPlayed / 50) + 1;
  $: xpInLevel = handsPlayed % 50;
  $: xpCurrent = 2460 + xpInLevel * 20;
  $: xpTotal = 5000;

  async function pickAvatar(id: string) {
    if (user) (user as any).avatar_id = id;
    await api.updateAvatar(id);
    await auth.refresh();
  }

  function pickFrame(frame: string) {
    if (user) (user as any).frame_id = frame;
    // TODO: save to backend later
  }
</script>

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Settings.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';
  import { auth } from '../stores/auth';
  import { cardTheme, CARD_THEMES } from '../stores/cardTheme';

  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};

  let sound = true;
  let music = false;
  let haptics = true;
  let animations = true;
  let graphics: 'ultra' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'performance' = 'high';
  let autoMuck = true;
  let fourColor = false;
  let betSlider = true;
  let autoPostBlinds = true;

  onMount(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('settings');
      if (saved) {
        const s = JSON.parse(saved);
        sound = s.sound ?? true;
        music = s.music ?? false;
        haptics = s.haptics ?? true;
        animations = s.animations ?? true;
        graphics = s.graphics ?? 'high';
        autoMuck = s.autoMuck ?? true;
        fourColor = s.fourColor ?? false;
        betSlider = s.betSlider ?? true;
        autoPostBlinds = s.autoPostBlinds ?? true;
      }
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  });

  function save() {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        'settings',
        JSON.stringify({
          sound, music, haptics, animations, graphics,
          autoMuck, fourColor, betSlider, autoPostBlinds,
        }),
      );
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }

  $: if (typeof window !== 'undefined') save();
</script>

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Shop.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};

  type Category = 'all' | 'chips' | 'cards' | 'frames' | 'table';
  type Rarity = 'common' | 'rare' | 'legendary' | 'immortal' | 'arcana' | 'ton';

  let activeCategory: Category = 'all';
  let rarityFilter: Rarity | 'all' = 'all';
  let priceFilter: 'all' | 'low' | 'high' = 'all';

  interface Item {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    category: Category;
    rarity: Rarity;
    priceUsd: number;
    priceTon: number;
    image: string;
    imageBg: string;
    label: string;
  }

  const items: Item[] = [
    { id: 'chip_blue',   name: 'Blue Chip Pack',   category: 'chips',  rarity: 'common',    priceUsd: 0.99,  priceTon: 0.25,  image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_blue',   label: 'Common' },
    { id: 'chip_green',  name: 'Green Chip Pack',  category: 'chips',  rarity: 'rare',      priceUsd: 4.99,  priceTon: 1.25,  image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_green',  label: 'Rare' },
    { id: 'chip_gold',   name: 'Gold Chip Pack',   category: 'chips',  rarity: 'legendary', priceUsd: 19.99, priceTon: 5.00,  image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_gold',   label: 'Legendary' },
    { id: 'chip_purple', name: 'Purple Chip Pack', category: 'chips',  rarity: 'immortal',  priceUsd: 49.99, priceTon: 12.50, image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_purple', label: 'Immortal' },
    { id: 'chip_red',    name: 'Red Chip Pack',    category: 'chips',  rarity: 'arcana',    priceUsd: 99.99, priceTon: 25.00, image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_red',    label: 'Arcana' },
    { id: 'chip_ton',    name: 'TON Chip Pack',    category: 'chips',  rarity: 'ton',       priceUsd: 99.99, priceTon: 25.00, image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_ton',    label: 'TON' },
    { id: 'card_royal',  name: 'Royal Deck',       category: 'cards',  rarity: 'legendary', priceUsd: 4.99,  priceTon: 1.25,  image: '/card-back-royal.png',   imageBg: 'card_royal',   label: 'Legendary' },
    { id: 'card_classic',name: 'Classic Deck',     category: 'cards',  rarity: 'rare',      priceUsd: 2.99,  priceTon: 0.75,  image: '/card-back-classic.png', imageBg: 'card_classic', label: 'Rare' },
    { id: 'card_dark',   name: 'Dark Deck',        category: 'cards',  rarity: 'immortal',  priceUsd: 6.99,  priceTon: 1.75,  image: '/card-back-dark.png',    imageBg: 'card_dark',    label: 'Immortal' },
    { id: 'frame_royal', name: 'Royal Frame',      category: 'frames', rarity: 'legendary', priceUsd: 9.99,  priceTon: 2.50,  image: '/frame-royal.png',       imageBg: 'frame_royal',  label: 'Legendary' },
    { id: 'frame_epic',  name: 'Epic Frame',       category: 'frames', rarity: 'rare',      priceUsd: 3.99,  priceTon: 1.00,  image: '/frame-epic.png',        imageBg: 'frame_epic',   label: 'Rare' },
    { id: 'table_royal', name: 'Royal Table',      category: 'table',  rarity: 'immortal',  priceUsd: 12.99, priceTon: 3.25,  image: '/table-top.png',         imageBg: 'table_royal',  label: 'Immortal' },
  ];

  const categories: { key: Category; label: string }[] = [
    { key: 'all', label: 'All' },
    { key: 'chips', label: 'Chips' },
    { key: 'cards', label: 'Cards' },
    { key: 'frames', label: 'Frames' },
    { key: 'table', label: 'Table' },
  ];

  const rarityColors: Record<string, string> = {
    common: '#94a3b8',
    rare: '#3b82f6',
    legendary: '#d4af37',
    immortal: '#8b5cf6',
    arcana: '#ef4444',
    ton: '#22d3ee',
  };

  // Chip background positions (for chips-set-hq.png 4×3 grid)
  const chipBg: Record<string, string> = {
    chip_blue:   '0% 0%',    // 1 chip
    chip_green:  '33.33% 0%', // 5 chip
    chip_gold:   '66.66% 0%', // 25 chip
    chip_purple: '100% 0%',  // 100 chip
    chip_red:    '0% 50%',   // 500 chip
    chip_ton:    '33.33% 50%', // 1K chip
  };

  $: filtered = items.filter((i) => {
    if (activeCategory !== 'all' && i.category !== activeCategory) return false;
    if (rarityFilter !== 'all' && i.rarity !== rarityFilter) return false;
    if (priceFilter === 'low' && i.priceUsd > 5) return false;
    if (priceFilter === 'high' && i.priceUsd <= 5) return false;
    return true;
  });

  let balanceUsd = 2840;
  let balanceTon = 1.25;

  function getImageStyle(item: Item): string {
    if (item.category === 'chips') {
      const pos = chipBg[item.imageBg] || '0% 0%';
      return `
        background-image: url('${item.image}');
        background-size: 400% 300%;
        background-position: ${pos};
        background-repeat: no-repeat;
      `;
    }
    if (item.category === 'cards') {
      return `
        background-image: url('${item.image}');
        background-size: 55% auto;
        background-position: center;
        background-repeat: no-repeat;
        border-radius: 8px;
      `;
    }
    if (item.category === 'table') {
      return `
        background-image: url('${item.image}');
        background-size: 95% auto;
        background-position: center;
        background-repeat: no-repeat;
      `;
    }
    // frames
    return `
      background-image: url('${item.image}');
      background-size: 70% auto;
      background-position: center;
      background-repeat: no-repeat;
    `;
  }
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">

  <!-- HEADER -->
  <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 mb-4 px-4">

      style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.7)); border: 1.5px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);"
    >
      <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white flex-1">🛒 Shop</h1>
  </div>

  <!-- BALANCE BAR -->
  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 px-4">
    <div class="flex-1 rounded-2xl px-3 py-2 flex items-center gap-2"

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Splash.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';

  let visible = true;
  onMount(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      visible = false;
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('splash-done'));
    }, 3000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  });
</script>

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Table.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let initialVariant: string = 'all';
  export let initialMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = 'all';
  export let onOpenTable: (id: number) => void = () => {};

  let tables: any[] = [];
  let loading = true;
  let filterMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = initialMode;
  let filterVariant: string = initialVariant;

  onMount(async () => {
    const res = await api.tables();
    if (res.ok && res.data) tables = res.data.tables;
    loading = false;
  });

  $: filtered = tables.filter((tbl) => {
    const okMode = filterMode === 'all' || tbl.mode === filterMode;
    const okVariant = filterVariant === 'all' || tbl.variant === filterVariant;
    return okMode && okVariant;
  });

  // Determine chip icon based on blind level
  function chipColorForTable(tbl: any): string {
    const bb = Number(tbl.blinds?.bb || 0);
    if (tbl.mode === 'free') {
      if (bb <= 1) return '#94a3b8';
      if (bb <= 10) return '#3b82f6';
      return '#10b981';
    }
    if (bb <= 1) return '#f59e0b';
    if (bb <= 20) return '#dc2626';
    if (bb <= 100) return '#0a0a0a';
    if (bb <= 1000) return '#8b5cf6';
    return '#d4af37';
  }

  function variantLabel(v: string): string {
    if (v === 'holdem') return "Texas Hold'em";
    if (v === 'omaha4') return 'Omaha 4';
    if (v === 'omaha5') return 'Omaha 5';
    return v;
  }

  function formatBlinds(tbl: any): string {
    const sb = Number(tbl.blinds?.sb || 0);
    const bb = Number(tbl.blinds?.bb || 0);
    return `${sb} / ${bb}`;
  }

  function playersCount(tbl: any): string {
    const cur = tbl.live_players || 0;
    const max = tbl.maxSeats || tbl.seats || 9;
    return `${cur} / ${max}`;
  }
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <TopBar />

  <!-- HEADER -->
  <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 mb-4 px-4">
    <button
      aria-label="Back"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition"
      style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.7)); border: 1.5px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);"
    >
      <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white flex-1">Cash Games</h1>
  </div>

  <!-- VARIANT TABS -->
  <div class="flex gap-1.5 mb-3 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 px-4">
    {#each [
      { key: 'all', label: 'All' },
      { key: 'holdem', label: "Hold'em" },
      { key: 'omaha4', label: 'Omaha 4' },
      { key: 'omaha5', label: 'Omaha 5' },
    ] as v}
      <button
        on:click={() => (filterVariant = v.key)}
        class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap active:scale-95 transition-all uppercase tracking-wider"
        style="
          background: {filterVariant === v.key
            ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8 0%, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15 100%)'
            : 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9))'};
          color: {filterVariant === v.key ? '#0a0800' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)'};
          border: 1px solid {filterVariant === v.key ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.15)'};
          {filterVariant === v.key ? 'box-shadow: 0 2px 0 #7a560a, inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,235,170,0.4);' : ''}
        "
      >
        {v.label}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>
