========== apps\mini-app\src\App.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import { auth } from './stores/auth';
  import { initRealtime } from './lib/realtime';
  import Login from './routes/Login.svelte';
  import Home from './routes/Home.svelte';
  import Tables from './routes/Tables.svelte';
  import Wallet from './routes/Wallet.svelte';
  import Profile from './routes/Profile.svelte';
  import Table from './routes/Table.svelte';
  import Leaderboard from './routes/Leaderboard.svelte';
  import Events from './routes/Events.svelte';
  import Tournaments from './routes/Tournaments.svelte';
  import TournamentDetail from './routes/TournamentDetail.svelte';
  import Achievements from './routes/Achievements.svelte';
  import Shop from './routes/Shop.svelte';
  import Notifications from './routes/Notifications.svelte';
  import Settings from './routes/Settings.svelte';
  import HelpCenter from './routes/HelpCenter.svelte';
  import Admin from './routes/Admin.svelte';
  import MyTournaments from './routes/MyTournaments.svelte';
  import SideNav from './components/SideNav.svelte';
  import BottomNav from './components/BottomNav.svelte';
  import ToastStack from './components/ToastStack.svelte';
  import Splash from './routes/Splash.svelte';

  type Page =
    | 'home' | 'tables' | 'wallet' | 'profile' | 'table' | 'leaderboard'
    | 'events' | 'tournaments' | 'tournament-detail' | 'achievements'
    | 'my-tournaments' | 'shop' | 'notifications' | 'settings' | 'help' | 'admin';

  let currentPage: Page = 'home';
  let activeTableId = 1;
  let activeTournamentId: number | null = null;
  let tableSource: 'cash' | 'tournament' = 'cash';
  let tablesVariant: string = 'all';
  let tablesMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = 'all';
  let tablesKey = 0;

  $: hideNav =
    currentPage === 'table' ||
    currentPage === 'achievements' ||
    currentPage === 'my-tournaments' ||
    currentPage === 'shop' ||
    currentPage === 'notifications' ||
    currentPage === 'settings' ||
    currentPage === 'help' ||
    currentPage === 'admin';

  let splashElapsed = false;
  $: forceSplash = $auth.loading || !splashElapsed;

  onMount(async () => {
    setTimeout(() => { splashElapsed = true; }, 3000);
    await auth.init();
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token) initRealtime(token);
  });

  function navigate(page: Page) {
    currentPage = page;
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function openTable(id: number) {
    activeTableId = id;
    tableSource = 'cash';
    currentPage = 'table';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function openTournamentTable(tournamentId: number, virtualTableId: number) {
    activeTournamentId = tournamentId;
    activeTableId = virtualTableId;
    tableSource = 'tournament';
    currentPage = 'table';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function openTournament(id: number) {
    activeTournamentId = id;
    currentPage = 'tournament-detail';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function backFromTable() {
    if (tableSource === 'tournament' && activeTournamentId) {
      currentPage = 'tournament-detail';
    } else {
      navigate('tables');
    }
  }

  function quickSeat(variant: string, mode: 'free' | 'real') {
    tablesVariant = variant;
    tablesMode = mode;
    tablesKey++;
    currentPage = 'tables';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }
</script>

<!-- ============ OUTER BACKGROUND ============ -->
<div
  class="fixed inset-0 -z-10"
  style="background: #05080f;"
>
  <div
    class="absolute inset-0"
    style="
      background-image: url('/bg-pattern.png');
      background-size: 600px auto;
      background-repeat: repeat;
      opacity: 0.35;
    "
  ></div>
  <div
    class="absolute inset-0"
    style="
      background:
        radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% -10%, rgba(30,58,138,0.5) 0%, transparent 55%),
        radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 110%, rgba(212,175,55,0.12) 0%, transparent 55%),
        linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(7,14,27,0.55) 0%, rgba(5,8,15,0.9) 100%);
    "
  ></div>
</div>

{#if forceSplash}
  <Splash />
{:else if !$auth.user}
  <Login />
{:else}
  <div class="relative flex" style="min-height: 100dvh;">
    <!-- ============ SIDE NAV (desktop) ============ -->
    {#if !hideNav}
      <SideNav current={currentPage as any} onNavigate={navigate} />
    {/if}

    <!-- ============ MAIN CONTENT ============ -->
    <main
      class="flex-1 relative"
      class:with-side={!hideNav}
      style="min-height: 100dvh;"
    >
      <div class="mx-auto" style="max-width: 520px;">
        {#if currentPage === 'home'}
          <Home onNavigate={navigate} onQuickSeat={quickSeat} onOpenTable={openTable} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'tables'}
          {#key tablesKey}
            <Tables initialVariant={tablesVariant} initialMode={tablesMode} onOpenTable={openTable} />
          {/key}
        {:else if currentPage === 'wallet'}
          <Wallet />
        {:else if currentPage === 'profile'}
          <Profile onNavigate={navigate} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'table'}
          <Table tableId={activeTableId} onBack={backFromTable} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'leaderboard'}
          <Leaderboard />
        {:else if currentPage === 'events'}
          <Events onNavigate={navigate} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'tournaments'}
          <Tournaments onOpenTournament={openTournament} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'tournament-detail' && activeTournamentId !== null}
          <TournamentDetail
            tournamentId={activeTournamentId}
            onBack={() => navigate('tournaments')}
            onOpenTable={openTournamentTable}
          />
        {:else if currentPage === 'achievements'}
          <Achievements onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'my-tournaments'}
          <MyTournaments onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'shop'}
          <Shop onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'notifications'}
          <Notifications onBack={() => navigate('home')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'settings'}
          <Settings onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'help'}
          <HelpCenter onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'admin'}
          <Admin onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {/if}
      </div>
    </main>

    <!-- ============ BOTTOM NAV (mobile) ============ -->
    {#if !hideNav}
      <BottomNav current={currentPage as any} onNavigate={navigate} />
    {/if}

    <ToastStack />
  </div>
{/if}

<style>
  @media (min-width: 1024px) {
    :global(main.with-side) {
      margin-left: 240px;
    }
  }
  @media (max-width: 1023px) {
    :global(main.with-side) {
      padding-bottom: 90px;
    }
  }
</style>


========== apps\mini-app\src\main.ts ==========

import '@fontsource/sora/400.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/500.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/600.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/700.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/800.css';
import './app.css';
import './app.css';
import { mount } from 'svelte';
import App from './App.svelte';

// 🔴 TON Connect (optional — fails silently if blocked)
(async () => {
  try {
    const { TonConnectUI } = await import('@tonconnect/ui');

    const tonConnectUI = new TonConnectUI({
      manifestUrl: `${window.location.origin}/tonconnect-manifest.json`,
      uiPreferences: { theme: 'DARK' as any },
    });

    (window as any).tonConnectUI = tonConnectUI;
    console.log('[tonconnect] initialized');
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.warn('[tonconnect] init failed (network?):', e.message);
    (window as any).tonConnectUI = null;
  }
})();

// 🔴 Svelte 5 mount
const app = mount(App, {
  target: document.getElementById('app')!,
});

export default app;

========== apps\server\migrations\008_late_reg_level.sql ==========

-- Add late_reg_level to tournaments
-- Standard MTT uses "levels" not "minutes" for late registration.
-- 0 = no late reg (registration closes at start)

ALTER TABLE tournaments
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS late_reg_level INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

COMMENT ON COLUMN tournaments.late_reg_level IS 'Last level at which new players can register. 0 = no late reg.';

-- Index for the auto-close check
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_late_reg
  ON tournaments (status, current_level)
  WHERE status IN ('running', 'final_table');


========== apps\server\migrations\009_reentry_bounty.sql ==========

-- D5.3: Re-entry + Bounty

ALTER TABLE tournaments
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS allow_re_entry BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS max_re_entries INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_bounty BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS bounty_amount NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

ALTER TABLE tournament_entries
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS re_entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

ALTER TABLE tournament_players
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS bounty_earned NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

-- eliminated_by might already exist
DO $$ BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_name = 'tournament_players' AND column_name = 'eliminated_by'
  ) THEN
    ALTER TABLE tournament_players ADD COLUMN eliminated_by BIGINT;
  END IF;
END $$;


========== apps\server\migrations\010_satellites.sql ==========

-- D5.4: Satellites
ALTER TABLE tournaments
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS satellite_target_id BIGINT REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS seats_awarded INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS satellite_tickets (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  target_tournament_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  source_tournament_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  position INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  used BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  used_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  UNIQUE(target_tournament_id, source_tournament_id, position)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_satellite_tickets_user
  ON satellite_tickets(user_id, used, target_tournament_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_satellite_tickets_target
  ON satellite_tickets(target_tournament_id, used);


========== apps\server\migrations\011_tournament_templates.sql ==========

-- D6.1: Recurring Tournament Templates
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_templates (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  variant TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'holdem',
  mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'free',
  format TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'sng',
  speed TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'regular',
  buy_in NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  fee NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  starting_stack BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 5000,
  min_players INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
  max_players INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
  guaranteed NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  late_reg_min INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  late_reg_level INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  allow_re_entry BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  max_re_entries INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  is_bounty BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  bounty_amount NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  satellite_target_id BIGINT REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  seats_awarded INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  blind_structure_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  schedule_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'daily',
  schedule_time TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '14:00',
  schedule_day INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  next_run_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  last_run_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_by BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_templates_next_run
  ON tournament_templates (is_active, next_run_at)
  WHERE is_active = TRUE;


========== apps\server\migrations\012_seasons.sql ==========

-- D6.2: Seasons + Tournament Points

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seasons (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  start_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  end_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  prize_pool NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

ALTER TABLE tournaments
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS season_id BIGINT REFERENCES seasons(id);

ALTER TABLE tournament_entries
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS points_earned INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_season
  ON tournaments(season_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_entries_season_points
  ON tournament_entries(tournament_id, points_earned)
  WHERE points_earned > 0;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_seasons_one_active
  ON seasons (status) WHERE status = 'active';


========== apps\server\migrations\run-008.ts ==========

import { Pool } from 'pg';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import dotenv from 'dotenv';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(__dirname, '../../../.env') });

const db = new Pool({
  connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL || 'postgres://poker:pokerpass@localhost:5432/poker',
});

async function main() {
  const sqlPath = path.join(__dirname, '008_late_reg_level.sql');
  const sql = fs.readFileSync(sqlPath, 'utf-8');
  console.log('Running:', sqlPath);

  await db.query(sql);
  console.log('✅ Migration applied');

  const check = await db.query(
    `SELECT column_name, data_type, column_default
     FROM information_schema.columns
     WHERE table_name = 'tournaments' AND column_name = 'late_reg_level'`,
  );
  if (check.rows.length > 0) {
    console.log('✅ Column exists:', check.rows[0]);
  } else {
    console.log('❌ Column NOT found');
  }

  await db.end();
}

main().catch((e) => {
  console.error('FATAL:', e);
  process.exit(1);
});

========== apps\server\src\__tests__\auth.test.ts ==========

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { signToken, verifyToken } from '../auth.js';

const SECRET = 'test-secret';

describe('Auth — JWT', () => {
  describe('signToken', () => {
    it('creates a valid JWT string', () => {
      const token = signToken({ uid: 1, tg: 123 }, SECRET);
      expect(token).toBeTypeOf('string');
      expect(token.split('.')).toHaveLength(3);
    });

    it('embeds payload', () => {
      const token = signToken({ uid: 42, tg: 999 }, SECRET);
      const payload = verifyToken(token, SECRET);
      expect(payload.uid).toBe(42);
      expect(payload.tg).toBe(999);
    });

    it('sets exp in future', () => {
      const token = signToken({ uid: 1 }, SECRET, 3600);
      const payload = verifyToken(token, SECRET);
      const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
      expect(payload.exp).toBeGreaterThan(now);
    });
  });

  describe('verifyToken', () => {
    it('verifies valid token', () => {
      const token = signToken({ uid: 1 }, SECRET);
      const payload = verifyToken(token, SECRET);
      expect(payload).toBeTruthy();
      expect(payload.uid).toBe(1);
    });

    it('rejects token with wrong secret', () => {
      const token = signToken({ uid: 1 }, SECRET);
      const payload = verifyToken(token, 'wrong-secret');
      expect(payload).toBeNull();
    });

    it('rejects malformed token', () => {
      expect(verifyToken('not.a.token', SECRET)).toBeNull();
      expect(verifyToken('', SECRET)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('rejects expired token', () => {
      const token = signToken({ uid: 1 }, SECRET, -1);
      const payload = verifyToken(token, SECRET);
      expect(payload).toBeNull();
    });
  });
});



========== apps\server\src\anti-cheat-routes.ts ==========

import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { verifyToken } from './auth.js';

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET ?? 'dev_secret_change_me';
const ADMIN_SECRET = process.env.ADMIN_SECRET ?? 'admin_dev_secret';

function requireAdmin(req: any, reply: any): boolean {
  const auth = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!auth?.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    reply.code(401).send({ error: 'no_token' });
    return false;
  }
  const token = auth.slice(7);
  if (token === ADMIN_SECRET) return true;
  const payload = verifyToken(token, JWT_SECRET);
  if (!payload) {
    reply.code(401).send({ error: 'invalid_token' });
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

export async function registerAntiCheatRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, db: Pool) {
  app.get('/api/admin/anticheat/risky-users', async (req, reply) => {
    if (!requireAdmin(req, reply)) return;
    const res = await db.query(
      `SELECT pr.user_id, pr.score, pr.flags, pr.action_taken, pr.last_updated,
              u.display_name, u.telegram_id, u.is_banned
       FROM player_risk pr
       JOIN users u ON u.id = pr.user_id
       WHERE pr.score > 0
       ORDER BY pr.score DESC LIMIT 100`,
    );
    return { users: res.rows };
  });

  app.get('/api/admin/anticheat/events', async (req, reply) => {
    if (!requireAdmin(req, reply)) return;
    const { resolved } = req.query as any;
    const where =
      resolved === 'true'
        ? 'WHERE se.resolved = TRUE'
        : resolved === 'false'
          ? 'WHERE se.resolved = FALSE'
          : '';
    const res = await db.query(
      `SELECT se.id, se.user_id, se.other_user_id, se.type, se.severity,
              se.evidence, se.resolved, se.reviewer_note, se.created_at,
              u.display_name as user_name, u2.display_name as other_name
       FROM suspicious_events se
       JOIN users u ON u.id = se.user_id
       LEFT JOIN users u2 ON u2.id = se.other_user_id
       ${where}
       ORDER BY se.created_at DESC LIMIT 200`,
    );
    return { events: res.rows };
  });

  app.get<{ Params: { userId: string } }>(
    '/api/admin/anticheat/user/:userId/actions',
    async (req, reply) => {
      if (!requireAdmin(req, reply)) return;
      const res = await db.query(
        `SELECT * FROM player_actions WHERE user_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100`,
        [req.params.userId],
      );
      return { actions: res.rows };
    },
  );

  app.get<{ Params: { userId: string } }>(
    '/api/admin/anticheat/user/:userId/ips',
    async (req, reply) => {
      if (!requireAdmin(req, reply)) return;
      const res = await db.query(
        `SELECT ip, first_seen, last_seen FROM user_ips WHERE user_id = $1 ORDER BY last_seen DESC`,
        [req.params.userId],
      );
      return { ips: res.rows };
    },
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string }; Body: { note?: string } }>(
    '/api/admin/anticheat/events/:id/resolve',
    async (req, reply) => {
      if (!requireAdmin(req, reply)) return;
      await db.query(
        `UPDATE suspicious_events SET resolved = TRUE, reviewer_note = $1 WHERE id = $2`,
        [req.body?.note ?? null, req.params.id],
      );
      return { ok: true };
    },
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { userId: string }; Body: { ban: boolean; reason?: string } }>(
    '/api/admin/anticheat/user/:userId/ban',
    async (req, reply) => {
      if (!requireAdmin(req, reply)) return;
      const { ban, reason } = req.body;
      await db.query('UPDATE users SET is_banned = $1 WHERE id = $2', [ban, req.params.userId]);
      await db.query(
        `INSERT INTO suspicious_events (user_id, type, severity, evidence, resolved)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, TRUE)`,
        [
          req.params.userId,
          ban ? 'manual_ban' : 'manual_unban',
          10,
          JSON.stringify({ reason: reason ?? 'admin action' }),
        ],
      );
      return { ok: true };
    },
  );

  app.get('/api/admin/anticheat/stats', async (req, reply) => {
    if (!requireAdmin(req, reply)) return;
    const [total, byType, highRisk] = await Promise.all([
      db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM suspicious_events WHERE resolved = FALSE'),
      db.query(
        `SELECT type, COUNT(*) as count FROM suspicious_events WHERE resolved = FALSE GROUP BY type`,
      ),
      db.query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM player_risk WHERE score >= 65'),
    ]);
    return {
      unresolvedTotal: Number(total.rows[0].count),
      highRiskUsers: Number(highRisk.rows[0].count),
      byType: byType.rows,
    };
  });
}



========== apps\server\src\anti-cheat.ts ==========

import { Pool } from 'pg';

const THRESHOLDS = {
  // 🔴 Relaxed for testing / dev
  botFastActionMs: 150,
  botFastActionCount: 30,
  botStdDevMs: 50,
  sameIpTablesTogether: 6,
  sameDeviceTablesTogether: 4,
  minHandsTogether: 50,
  chipDumpingThreshold: 0.85,
  warnScore: 70,
  shadowbanScore: 90,
  banScore: 99, // effectively disable auto-ban during testing
};

export interface ActionRecord {
  userId: number;
  tableId: number;
  handNo: number;
  action: 'fold' | 'check' | 'call' | 'raise';
  amount: number;
  street: string;
  responseMs: number;
}

export async function recordIp(db: Pool, userId: number, ip: string) {
  if (!ip) return;
  try {
    await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO user_ips (user_id, ip) VALUES ($1, $2)
       ON CONFLICT (user_id, ip) DO UPDATE SET last_seen = NOW()`,
      [userId, ip],
    );
  } catch (e) {}
}

export async function recordDevice(db: Pool, userId: number, deviceHash: string) {
  if (!deviceHash) return;
  try {
    await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO user_devices (user_id, device_hash) VALUES ($1, $2)
       ON CONFLICT (user_id, device_hash) DO UPDATE SET last_seen = NOW()`,
      [userId, deviceHash],
    );
  } catch (e) {}
}

export async function recordAction(db: Pool, a: ActionRecord) {
  try {
    await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO player_actions (user_id, table_id, hand_no, action, amount, street, response_ms)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)`,
      [a.userId, a.tableId, a.handNo, a.action, a.amount, a.street, a.responseMs],
    );
  } catch (e) {}
}

export async function detectMultiAccount(
  db: Pool,
  userId: number,
  tableId: number,
): Promise<{ suspicious: boolean; severity: number; evidence: any }> {
  const res = await db.query(
    `SELECT ui2.user_id, ui.ip, COUNT(DISTINCT pa.hand_no) as shared_hands
     FROM user_ips ui
     JOIN user_ips ui2 ON ui2.ip = ui.ip AND ui2.user_id != ui.user_id
     JOIN player_actions pa ON pa.user_id = ui2.user_id AND pa.table_id = $1
     WHERE ui.user_id = $2 AND ui.last_seen > NOW() - INTERVAL '2 hours'
     GROUP BY ui2.user_id, ui.ip
     HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pa.hand_no) >= $3`,
    [tableId, userId, THRESHOLDS.sameIpTablesTogether],
  );

  if (res.rows.length > 0) {
    const suspects = res.rows.map((r) => ({
      userId: r.user_id,
      ip: r.ip,
      sharedHands: r.shared_hands,
    }));
    return { suspicious: true, severity: 8, evidence: { suspects } };
  }

  const devRes = await db.query(
    `SELECT ud2.user_id, ud.device_hash, COUNT(DISTINCT pa.hand_no) as shared_hands
     FROM user_devices ud
     JOIN user_devices ud2 ON ud2.device_hash = ud.device_hash AND ud2.user_id != ud.user_id
     JOIN player_actions pa ON pa.user_id = ud2.user_id AND pa.table_id = $1
     WHERE ud.user_id = $2 AND ud.last_seen > NOW() - INTERVAL '2 hours'
     GROUP BY ud2.user_id, ud.device_hash
     HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pa.hand_no) >= $3`,
    [tableId, userId, THRESHOLDS.sameDeviceTablesTogether],
  );

  if (devRes.rows.length > 0) {
    return { suspicious: true, severity: 10, evidence: { devices: devRes.rows } };
  }

  return { suspicious: false, severity: 0, evidence: {} };
}

export async function detectBot(
  db: Pool,
  userId: number,
): Promise<{
  suspicious: boolean;
  severity: number;
  evidence: any;
}> {
  const res = await db.query(
    `SELECT response_ms FROM player_actions
     WHERE user_id = $1 AND response_ms IS NOT NULL
     ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 50`,
    [userId],
  );

  if (res.rows.length < 15) return { suspicious: false, severity: 0, evidence: {} };

  const times = res.rows.map((r) => r.response_ms);
  const avg = times.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / times.length;
  const variance = times.reduce((a, b) => a + Math.pow(b - avg, 2), 0) / times.length;
  const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
  const fastCount = times.filter((t) => t < THRESHOLDS.botFastActionMs).length;

  if (fastCount >= THRESHOLDS.botFastActionCount) {
    return {
      suspicious: true,
      severity: 7,
      evidence: { fastCount, avgMs: Math.round(avg), stdDev: Math.round(stdDev) },
    };
  }
  if (stdDev < THRESHOLDS.botStdDevMs && avg < 1500) {
    return {
      suspicious: true,
      severity: 6,
      evidence: { avgMs: Math.round(avg), stdDev: Math.round(stdDev) },
    };
  }

  return { suspicious: false, severity: 0, evidence: {} };
}

export async function detectChipDumping(
  db: Pool,
  userId: number,
): Promise<{ suspicious: boolean; severity: number; evidence: any }> {
  const res = await db.query(
    `SELECT user_a, user_b, hands_together, chips_net_flow
     FROM player_pairs
     WHERE (user_a = $1 OR user_b = $1)
       AND hands_together >= $2
       AND ABS(chips_net_flow) > 100
     ORDER BY ABS(chips_net_flow) DESC LIMIT 5`,
    [userId, THRESHOLDS.minHandsTogether],
  );

  for (const row of res.rows) {
    const other = row.user_a === userId ? row.user_b : row.user_a;
    const netFromMe = row.user_a === userId ? row.chips_net_flow : -row.chips_net_flow;
    if (netFromMe > 0) {
      const ratio = netFromMe / Math.abs(row.chips_net_flow);
      if (ratio >= THRESHOLDS.chipDumpingThreshold) {
        return {
          suspicious: true,
          severity: 9,
          evidence: {
            otherUserId: other,
            handsTogether: row.hands_together,
            chipsTransferred: Math.round(netFromMe * 100) / 100,
            ratio: Math.round(ratio * 100) / 100,
          },
        };
      }
    }
  }

  return { suspicious: false, severity: 0, evidence: {} };
}

export async function reportSuspicious(
  db: Pool,
  userId: number,
  otherUserId: number | null,
  type: string,
  severity: number,
  evidence: any,
) {
  try {
    const existing = await db.query(
      `SELECT id FROM suspicious_events
       WHERE user_id = $1 AND type = $2 AND resolved = FALSE
         AND created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours'
       LIMIT 1`,
      [userId, type],
    );
    if (existing.rows.length > 0) return;

    await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO suspicious_events (user_id, other_user_id, type, severity, evidence)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
      [userId, otherUserId, type, severity, JSON.stringify(evidence)],
    );

    console.log(`[anti-cheat] 🚨 ${type} detected for user ${userId} (severity ${severity})`);
    await updateRiskScore(db, userId);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[anti-cheat] reportSuspicious error', e);
  }
}

export async function updateRiskScore(db: Pool, userId: number): Promise<number> {
  const events = await db.query(
    `SELECT type, severity FROM suspicious_events
     WHERE user_id = $1 AND resolved = FALSE AND created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'`,
    [userId],
  );

  let score = 0;
  const flags: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const e of events.rows) {
    score += e.severity * 5;
    flags[e.type] = (flags[e.type] || 0) + e.severity;
  }
  score = Math.min(score, 100);

  let action = 'none';
  if (score >= THRESHOLDS.banScore) action = 'ban';
  else if (score >= THRESHOLDS.shadowbanScore) action = 'shadowban';
  else if (score >= THRESHOLDS.warnScore) action = 'warn';

  try {
    await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO player_risk (user_id, score, flags, action_taken, last_updated)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, NOW())
       ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
       SET score = $2, flags = $3, action_taken = $4, last_updated = NOW()`,
      [userId, score, JSON.stringify(flags), action],
    );
    if (action === 'ban') {
      await db.query('UPDATE users SET is_banned = TRUE WHERE id = $1', [userId]);
      console.log(`[anti-cheat] 🚫 user ${userId} auto-banned (score ${score})`);
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  return score;
}

export async function analyzeUser(db: Pool, userId: number, tableId: number): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const [multi, bot, dump] = await Promise.all([
      detectMultiAccount(db, userId, tableId),
      detectBot(db, userId),
      detectChipDumping(db, userId),
    ]);

    if (multi.suspicious) {
      await reportSuspicious(
        db,
        userId,
        multi.evidence?.suspects?.[0]?.userId ?? null,
        'multi_account',
        multi.severity,
        multi.evidence,
      );
    }
    if (bot.suspicious) {
      await reportSuspicious(db, userId, null, 'bot', bot.severity, bot.evidence);
    }
    if (dump.suspicious) {
      await reportSuspicious(
        db,
        userId,
        dump.evidence?.otherUserId ?? null,
        'chip_dumping',
        dump.severity,
        dump.evidence,
      );
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[anti-cheat] analyzeUser error', e);
  }
}


========== apps\server\src\auth.ts ==========

import crypto from 'crypto';

export interface TelegramUser {
  id: number;
  first_name: string;
  last_name?: string;
  username?: string;
  language_code?: string;
  is_premium?: boolean;
}

export interface ParsedInitData {
  user: TelegramUser;
  authDate: number;
  hash: string;
  queryId?: string;
}

export function verifyTelegramInitData(initData: string, botToken: string): ParsedInitData | null {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(initData);
  const hash = params.get('hash');
  if (!hash) return null;

  params.delete('hash');

  const dataCheckString = [...params.entries()]
    .map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`)
    .sort()
    .join('\n');

  const secretKey = crypto.createHmac('sha256', 'WebAppData').update(botToken).digest();

  const calculatedHash = crypto
    .createHmac('sha256', secretKey)
    .update(dataCheckString)
    .digest('hex');

  if (calculatedHash !== hash) return null;

  const userJson = params.get('user');
  if (!userJson) return null;

  const user: TelegramUser = JSON.parse(userJson);
  const authDate = Number(params.get('auth_date') ?? 0);

  if (Date.now() / 1000 - authDate > 86400) return null;

  return {
    user,
    authDate,
    hash,
    queryId: params.get('query_id') ?? undefined,
  };
}

export function signToken(payload: object, secret: string, expiresInSec = 604800): string {
  const header = { alg: 'HS256', typ: 'JWT' };
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const body = { ...payload, iat: now, exp: now + expiresInSec };

  const b64 = (o: object) => Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(o)).toString('base64url');

  const h = b64(header);
  const p = b64(body);
  const sig = crypto.createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`${h}.${p}`).digest('base64url');

  return `${h}.${p}.${sig}`;
}

export function verifyToken(token: string, secret: string): any | null {
  try {
    const [h, p, sig] = token.split('.');
    const expected = crypto.createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`${h}.${p}`).digest('base64url');
    if (sig !== expected) return null;

    const payload = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(p, 'base64url').toString());
    if (payload.exp < Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)) return null;
    return payload;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}



========== apps\server\src\blind-structures.ts ==========

import { Pool } from 'pg';

export interface BlindLevel {
  level: number;
  sb: number;
  bb: number;
  ante: number;
  durationSec: number;
  isBreak: boolean;
}

export class BlindStructureService {
  private cache: Map<number, BlindLevel[]> = new Map();

  constructor(private db: Pool) {}

  async getLevels(structureId: number): Promise<BlindLevel[]> {
    if (this.cache.has(structureId)) {
      return this.cache.get(structureId)!;
    }

    const res = await this.db.query(
      `SELECT level, small_blind, big_blind, ante, duration_sec, is_break
       FROM blind_levels
       WHERE structure_id = $1
       ORDER BY level ASC`,
      [structureId],
    );

    const levels: BlindLevel[] = res.rows.map((r) => ({
      level: r.level,
      sb: Number(r.small_blind),
      bb: Number(r.big_blind),
      ante: Number(r.ante),
      durationSec: r.duration_sec,
      isBreak: r.is_break,
    }));

    this.cache.set(structureId, levels);
    return levels;
  }

  async getLevel(structureId: number, level: number): Promise<BlindLevel | null> {
    const levels = await this.getLevels(structureId);
    return levels.find((l) => l.level === level) ?? null;
  }

  /**
   * Compute the current level based on elapsed seconds since tournament start
   */
  async getLevelAtTime(
    structureId: number,
    elapsedSec: number,
  ): Promise<{
    level: number;
    levelData: BlindLevel | null;
    nextLevelIn: number;
  }> {
    const levels = await this.getLevels(structureId);
    if (levels.length === 0) {
      return { level: 1, levelData: null, nextLevelIn: 0 };
    }

    let elapsed = 0;
    for (const lvl of levels) {
      if (elapsedSec < elapsed + lvl.durationSec) {
        return {
          level: lvl.level,
          levelData: lvl,
          nextLevelIn: Math.max(0, elapsed + lvl.durationSec - elapsedSec),
        };
      }
      elapsed += lvl.durationSec;
    }

    // Past last level — return last
    const last = levels[levels.length - 1];
    return { level: last.level, levelData: last, nextLevelIn: 0 };
  }

  invalidateCache(structureId: number): void {
    this.cache.delete(structureId);
  }
}



========== apps\server\src\command-queue.ts ==========

import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { ErrorCode, type CommandResult } from './errors.js';

export class CommandQueue {
  constructor(private db: Pool) {}

  /**
   * اجرای idempotent یک command
   * - اگه commandId تکراری باشه، نتیجه‌ی قبلی برمی‌گرده
   * - اگه جدید باشه، handler اجرا می‌شه و نتیجه ذخیره می‌شه
   */
  async run<T>(
    commandId: string,
    userId: number,
    scope: string,
    handler: () => Promise<CommandResult<T>>,
  ): Promise<CommandResult<T>> {
    if (!commandId) {
      // اگه commandId نباشه، idempotency نداره
      return handler();
    }

    // 1. چک کن قبلاً پردازش شده؟
    try {
      const existing = await this.db.query('SELECT result FROM idempotency_keys WHERE key = $1', [
        commandId,
      ]);

      if (existing.rows.length > 0) {
        return {
          ok: true,
          duplicate: true,
          data: existing.rows[0].result?.data,
        };
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[command-queue] check failed:', e.message);
    }

    // 2. اجرا کن
    const result = await handler();

    // 3. ذخیره کن
    try {
      await this.db.query(
        `INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (key, user_id, scope, result, expires_at)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, NOW() + INTERVAL '1 hour')
         ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING`,
        [commandId, userId, scope, JSON.stringify({ data: result.data ?? null })],
      );
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[command-queue] save failed:', e.message);
    }

    return result;
  }
}



========== apps\server\src\deposit-watcher.ts ==========

import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.js';

const TON_NETWORK = process.env.TON_NETWORK || 'testnet';
const TON_API_KEY = process.env.TON_API_KEY || '';
const TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS = process.env.TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS || '';
const TON_WITHDRAW_FEE = Number(process.env.TON_WITHDRAW_FEE || 0.05);
const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 15_000;
const TON_PRICE_USD = Number(process.env.TON_PRICE_USD || 5);

const API_BASE =
  TON_NETWORK === 'mainnet'
    ? 'https://toncenter.com/api/v2'
    : 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2';

export class DepositWatcher {
  private ledger: LedgerService;
  private timer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
  private running = false;

  constructor(private db: Pool) {
    this.ledger = new LedgerService(db);
  }

  start(): void {
    console.log(
      `[deposit-watcher] starting. address=${TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS} interval=${POLL_INTERVAL_MS}ms`,
    );
    this.tick();
    this.timer = setInterval(() => this.tick(), POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearInterval(this.timer);
      this.timer = null;
    }
  }

  private async tick(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.running) return;
    this.running = true;
    try {
      await this.checkDeposits();
      await this.checkPendingWithdrawals();
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[deposit-watcher] tick failed:', e.message);
    } finally {
      this.running = false;
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // DEPOSITS
  // ============================================================
  private async checkDeposits(): Promise<void> {
    if (!TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS) return;
    const txs = await this.fetchTransactions(TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS);
    for (const tx of txs) {
      await this.processDeposit(tx);
    }
  }

  private async fetchTransactions(address: string): Promise<any[]> {
    try {
      const url = `${API_BASE}/getTransactions?address=${encodeURIComponent(address)}&limit=30${TON_API_KEY ? '&api_key=' + TON_API_KEY : ''}`;
      const res = await fetch(url);
      if (!res.ok) {
        console.error('[deposit-watcher] API error:', res.status);
        return [];
      }
      const data: any = await res.json();
      return data.result || [];
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[deposit-watcher] fetch failed:', e.message);
      return [];
    }
  }

  private async processDeposit(tx: any): Promise<void> {
    const txHash = tx.transaction_id?.hash;
    if (!txHash) return;

    const existing = await this.db.query('SELECT id FROM ton_transactions WHERE tx_hash = $1', [
      txHash,
    ]);
    if (existing.rows.length > 0) return;

    const inMsg = tx.in_msg;
    if (!inMsg || !inMsg.value || Number(inMsg.value) <= 0) return;

    const memo = String(inMsg.message || '').trim();
    const fromAddress = inMsg.source || 'unknown';
    const amountTon = Number(inMsg.value) / 1e9;
    const txTime = new Date(Number(tx.utime || 0) * 1000);

    const userId = Number(memo);
    if (!userId || isNaN(userId)) {
      console.warn(`[deposit-watcher] ignoring tx ${txHash.slice(0, 12)}…: invalid memo "${memo}"`);
      return;
    }

    const userRes = await this.db.query('SELECT id FROM users WHERE id = $1', [userId]);
    if (userRes.rows.length === 0) {
      console.warn(`[deposit-watcher] unknown user id=${userId}`);
      return;
    }

    const amountUsd = Math.round(amountTon * TON_PRICE_USD * 100) / 100;
    console.log(
      `[deposit-watcher] 💰 deposit: user=${userId} amount=${amountTon} TON ($${amountUsd})`,
    );

    try {
      const tonRes = await this.db.query(
        `INSERT INTO ton_transactions
           (user_id, direction, amount_ton, amount_usd, tx_hash, from_address, to_address, memo, status, confirmations, confirmed_at)
         VALUES ($1, 'deposit', $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, 'confirmed', 1, NOW())
         ON CONFLICT (tx_hash) DO NOTHING
         RETURNING id`,
        [userId, amountTon, amountUsd, txHash, fromAddress, TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS, memo],
      );

      if (tonRes.rows.length === 0) return;
      const tonTxId = tonRes.rows[0].id;

      const userAcc = await this.ledger.getUserAccountId(userId);
      const depositAcc = await this.ledger.getAccountId('SYSTEM_TON_DEPOSITS');
      if (!userAcc || !depositAcc) return;

      const ledgerResult = await this.ledger.record({
        kind: 'DEPOSIT',
        userId,
        ref: `ton_${txHash}`,
        meta: { txHash, amountTon, fromAddress, memo },
        entries: [
          { accountId: depositAcc, amount: -amountUsd },
          { accountId: userAcc, amount: amountUsd },
        ],
        idempotencyKey: `ton_dep_${txHash}`,
      });

      if (!ledgerResult.ok) return;

      await this.db.query('UPDATE ton_transactions SET ledger_tx_id = $1 WHERE id = $2', [
        ledgerResult.transactionId,
        tonTxId,
      ]);

      console.log(`[deposit-watcher] ✅ credited user=${userId} $${amountUsd}`);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(`[deposit-watcher] process failed: ${e.message}`);
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 🔴 WITHDRAWAL CONFIRMATION
  // ============================================================
  private async checkPendingWithdrawals(): Promise<void> {
    const pending = await this.db.query(
      `SELECT id, user_id, amount_ton, amount_usd, to_address, created_at
       FROM ton_transactions
       WHERE direction = 'withdraw'
         AND status IN ('processing', 'pending')
         AND created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '1 hour'
       ORDER BY id DESC
       LIMIT 20`,
    );

    if (pending.rows.length === 0) return;

    for (const tx of pending.rows) {
      await this.verifyWithdrawal(tx);
    }
  }

  private async verifyWithdrawal(tx: any): Promise<void> {
    const amountSent = Number(tx.amount_ton) - TON_WITHDRAW_FEE;
    const toAddress = tx.to_address;
    const createdAt = new Date(tx.created_at).getTime() / 1000;

    // 🔴 Query destination address transactions
    const txs = await this.fetchTransactions(toAddress);

    // Match by amount + timestamp proximity
    for (const t of txs) {
      const inMsg = t.in_msg;
      if (!inMsg || !inMsg.value) continue;

      const inValue = Number(inMsg.value) / 1e9;
      const txTime = Number(t.utime || 0);

      // 🔴 Match: amount within 5% + timestamp within 5 min
      const amountMatch = Math.abs(inValue - amountSent) < 0.005;
      const timeMatch = Math.abs(txTime - createdAt) < 300;

      if (amountMatch && timeMatch) {
        console.log(`[deposit-watcher] ✅ withdrawal confirmed: id=${tx.id} amount=${inValue} TON`);
        await this.db.query(
          `UPDATE ton_transactions SET status = 'confirmed', confirmed_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`,
          [tx.id],
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    // 🔴 Timeout: if older than 10 min and not found → refund
    const ageMin = (Date.now() / 1000 - createdAt) / 60;
    if (ageMin > 10 && tx.status === 'processing') {
      console.warn(
        `[deposit-watcher] ⏱️ withdrawal timeout: id=${tx.id} age=${ageMin.toFixed(1)}min`,
      );
      await this.refundWithdrawal(tx);
    }
  }

  private async refundWithdrawal(tx: any): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const userId = tx.user_id;
      const amountUsd = Number(tx.amount_usd || 0);

      const userAcc = await this.ledger.getUserAccountId(userId);
      const escrowAcc = await this.ledger.getAccountId('SYSTEM_ESCROW');
      if (!userAcc || !escrowAcc) return;

      const result = await this.ledger.record({
        kind: 'WITHDRAWAL',
        userId,
        ref: `withdraw_refund_${tx.id}`,
        meta: { originalTxId: tx.id, reason: 'timeout' },
        entries: [
          { accountId: escrowAcc, amount: -amountUsd },
          { accountId: userAcc, amount: amountUsd },
        ],
        idempotencyKey: `wd_refund_${tx.id}`,
      });

      if (result.ok) {
        await this.db.query(
          `UPDATE ton_transactions SET status = 'failed', error = 'timeout_no_confirmation' WHERE id = $1`,
          [tx.id],
        );
        console.log(`[deposit-watcher] 💸 refunded user=${userId} $${amountUsd}`);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(`[deposit-watcher] refund failed: ${e.message}`);
    }
  }
}

========== apps\server\src\errors.ts ==========

export const ErrorCode = {
  // Auth
  AUTH_REQUIRED: 'AUTH_REQUIRED',
  INVALID_SESSION: 'INVALID_SESSION',

  // Table
  TABLE_NOT_FOUND: 'TABLE_NOT_FOUND',
  SEAT_UNAVAILABLE: 'SEAT_UNAVAILABLE',
  NOT_SEATED: 'NOT_SEATED',
  NOT_IN_ROOM: 'NOT_IN_ROOM',
  NOT_YOUR_TURN: 'NOT_YOUR_TURN',
  NOT_ACTIVE: 'NOT_ACTIVE',

  // Game
  INVALID_ACTION: 'INVALID_ACTION',
  HAND_NOT_ACTIVE: 'HAND_NOT_ACTIVE',
  CANNOT_CHECK: 'CANNOT_CHECK',
  RAISE_TOO_SMALL: 'RAISE_TOO_SMALL',
  RAISE_BELOW_CURRENT: 'RAISE_BELOW_CURRENT',
  NOT_ENOUGH_STACK: 'NOT_ENOUGH_STACK',
  BAD_ACTION: 'BAD_ACTION',
  BAD_SEAT: 'BAD_SEAT',

  // Money
  INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE: 'INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE',
  PAYMENT_FAILED: 'PAYMENT_FAILED',
  BUY_IN_FAILED: 'BUY_IN_FAILED',

  // Command
  COMMAND_ALREADY_PROCESSED: 'COMMAND_ALREADY_PROCESSED',
  STALE_STATE: 'STALE_STATE',
  RESYNC_REQUIRED: 'RESYNC_REQUIRED',

  // Rate
  RATE_LIMITED: 'RATE_LIMITED',
} as const;

export type ErrorCodeType = (typeof ErrorCode)[keyof typeof ErrorCode];

export interface CommandResult<T = any> {
  ok: boolean;
  error?: string;
  code?: ErrorCodeType;
  data?: T;
  duplicate?: boolean;
}

export class AppError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public code: ErrorCodeType,
    message?: string,
  ) {
    super(message || code);
    this.name = 'AppError';
  }
}



========== apps\server\src\events.ts ==========

import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { verifyToken } from './auth.js';

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET ?? 'dev_secret_change_me';
const ADMIN_SECRET = process.env.ADMIN_SECRET ?? 'admin_dev_secret';

function authUser(req: any): number | null {
  const auth = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!auth?.startsWith('Bearer ')) return null;
  const payload = verifyToken(auth.slice(7), JWT_SECRET);
  return payload?.uid ?? null;
}

function isAdmin(req: any): boolean {
  const auth = req.headers.authorization;
  return auth === `Bearer ${ADMIN_SECRET}`;
}

async function logEvent(db: Pool, eventId: number, type: string, message: string, meta: any = {}) {
  try {
    await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO event_logs (event_id, type, message, meta) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)`,
      [eventId, type, message, JSON.stringify(meta)],
    );
  } catch (e) {}
}

export async function registerEventsRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, db: Pool) {
  app.get('/api/events', async () => {
    const res = await db.query(
      `SELECT e.*,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM event_registrations er WHERE er.event_id = e.id) as registered_count
       FROM events e
       WHERE e.status IN ('scheduled', 'registering', 'running')
         AND e.starts_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '1 day'
       ORDER BY
         CASE e.status WHEN 'running' THEN 1 WHEN 'registering' THEN 2 WHEN 'scheduled' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END,
         e.starts_at ASC`,
    );
    return { events: res.rows };
  });

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/api/events/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const evRes = await db.query('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = $1', [id]);
    if (evRes.rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'event_not_found' });
    const regs = await db.query(
      `SELECT er.user_id, u.display_name, u.avatar_id, er.registered_at
       FROM event_registrations er
       JOIN users u ON u.id = er.user_id
       WHERE er.event_id = $1 ORDER BY er.registered_at ASC LIMIT 200`,
      [id],
    );
    const results = await db.query(
      `SELECT tr.*, u.display_name, u.avatar_id
       FROM tournament_results tr
       JOIN users u ON u.id = tr.user_id
       WHERE tr.event_id = $1 ORDER BY tr.rank ASC LIMIT 100`,
      [id],
    );
    return { event: evRes.rows[0], registrations: regs.rows, results: results.rows };
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/api/events/:id/register', async (req, reply) => {
    const userId = authUser(req);
    if (!userId) return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'unauthorized' });
    const { id } = req.params;
    const evRes = await db.query('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = $1', [id]);
    if (evRes.rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'event_not_found' });
    const ev = evRes.rows[0];
    if (!['scheduled', 'registering'].includes(ev.status))
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'event_not_open' });
    const cntRes = await db.query(
      'SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM event_registrations WHERE event_id = $1',
      [id],
    );
    if (ev.max_players && Number(cntRes.rows[0].c) >= ev.max_players)
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'event_full' });
    if (Number(ev.buyin_usd) > 0) {
      const wRes = await db.query('SELECT balance_usd FROM wallets WHERE user_id = $1', [userId]);
      if (wRes.rows.length === 0 || Number(wRes.rows[0].balance_usd) < Number(ev.buyin_usd)) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'insufficient_balance' });
      }
    }
    try {
      await db.query('INSERT INTO event_registrations (event_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)', [
        id,
        userId,
      ]);
      if (Number(ev.buyin_usd) > 0) {
        await db.query('UPDATE wallets SET balance_usd = balance_usd - $1 WHERE user_id = $2', [
          ev.buyin_usd,
          userId,
        ]);
        const toPrize = Number(ev.buyin_usd) * 0.9;
        await db.query(
          `UPDATE events SET prize_pool_collected = COALESCE(prize_pool_collected, 0) + $1 WHERE id = $2`,
          [toPrize, id],
        );
        const rakeAmount = Number(ev.buyin_usd) * 0.1;
        const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        await db.query(
          `INSERT INTO revenue_daily (date, rake_total) VALUES ($1, $2)
           ON CONFLICT (date) DO UPDATE SET rake_total = revenue_daily.rake_total + $2`,
          [today, rakeAmount],
        );
      }
      await logEvent(db, Number(id), 'register', `User ${userId} registered`, { userId });
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (e.code === '23505') return { ok: true, already: true };
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'register_failed' });
    }
  });

  app.delete<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/api/events/:id/register', async (req, reply) => {
    const userId = authUser(req);
    if (!userId) return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'unauthorized' });
    const { id } = req.params;
    const evRes = await db.query('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = $1', [id]);
    if (evRes.rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'event_not_found' });
    const ev = evRes.rows[0];
    if (ev.status === 'running' || ev.status === 'finished')
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'event_started' });
    const delRes = await db.query(
      'DELETE FROM event_registrations WHERE event_id = $1 AND user_id = $2 RETURNING id',
      [id, userId],
    );
    if (delRes.rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'not_registered' });
    if (Number(ev.buyin_usd) > 0) {
      const refund = Number(ev.buyin_usd);
      await db.query('UPDATE wallets SET balance_usd = balance_usd + $1 WHERE user_id = $2', [
        refund,
        userId,
      ]);
      await db.query(
        `UPDATE events SET prize_pool_collected = COALESCE(prize_pool_collected, 0) - $1 WHERE id = $2`,
        [refund * 0.9, id],
      );
    }
    await logEvent(db, Number(id), 'unregister', `User ${userId} unregistered`, { userId });
    return { ok: true };
  });

  app.post<{ Body: Record<string, any> }>('/api/admin/events', async (req, reply) => {
    if (!isAdmin(req)) return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'forbidden' });
    const b: Record<string, any> = req.body ?? {};
    if (!b.title || !b.variant || !b.starts_at)
      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'missing_fields' });
    const blind =
      b.blind_structure ??
      (await db.query('SELECT default_blind_structure($1) as s', [b.starting_stack ?? 1500]))
        .rows[0].s;
    const prize =
      b.prize_structure ?? (await db.query('SELECT default_prize_structure() as s')).rows[0].s;
    const inserted = await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO events (title, variant, starts_at, buyin_usd, prize_pool_usd, max_players,
        min_players, status, blind_structure, prize_structure, starting_stack, late_reg_level, prize_pool_collected)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, 'scheduled', $8, $9, $10, $11, $12) RETURNING *`,
      [
        b.title,
        b.variant,
        new Date(b.starts_at),
        b.buyin_usd ?? 0,
        0,
        b.max_players ?? 100,
        b.min_players ?? 2,
        JSON.stringify(blind),
        JSON.stringify(prize),
        b.starting_stack ?? 1500,
        b.late_reg_level ?? 3,
        0,
      ],
    );
    await logEvent(db, inserted.rows[0].id, 'created', `Tournament created by admin`);
    return { ok: true, event: inserted.rows[0] };
  });

  app.get('/api/admin/events', async (req, reply) => {
    if (!isAdmin(req)) return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'forbidden' });
    const res = await db.query(
      `SELECT e.*, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM event_registrations er WHERE er.event_id = e.id) as reg_count
       FROM events e ORDER BY e.starts_at DESC LIMIT 100`,
    );
    return { events: res.rows };
  });

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string }; Body: { reason?: string } }>(
    '/api/admin/events/:id/cancel',
    async (req, reply) => {
      if (!isAdmin(req)) return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'forbidden' });
      const { id } = req.params;
      const { reason } = req.body ?? {};
      const evRes = await db.query('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = $1', [id]);
      if (evRes.rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'event_not_found' });
      const ev = evRes.rows[0];
      if (['finished', 'cancelled'].includes(ev.status))
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'already_done' });
      if (Number(ev.buyin_usd) > 0) {
        const regs = await db.query('SELECT user_id FROM event_registrations WHERE event_id = $1', [
          id,
        ]);
        for (const r of regs.rows) {
          await db.query('UPDATE wallets SET balance_usd = balance_usd + $1 WHERE user_id = $2', [
            ev.buyin_usd,
            r.user_id,
          ]);
        }
      }
      await db.query(
        `UPDATE events SET status = 'cancelled', cancelled_reason = $1 WHERE id = $2`,
        [reason ?? 'cancelled by admin', id],
      );
      await logEvent(db, Number(id), 'cancelled', reason ?? 'cancelled by admin');
      return { ok: true };
    },
  );

  app.post<{ Params: { id: string }; Body: { status: string } }>(
    '/api/admin/events/:id/status',
    async (req, reply) => {
      if (!isAdmin(req)) return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'forbidden' });
      const { id } = req.params;
      const { status } = req.body ?? {};
      if (!['scheduled', 'registering', 'running', 'finished'].includes(status)) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'invalid_status' });
      }
      await db.query(`UPDATE events SET status = $1 WHERE id = $2`, [status, id]);
      await logEvent(db, Number(id), 'status_change', `Status changed to ${status}`);
      if (status === 'running') {
        await db.query(
          `UPDATE events SET level_started_at = NOW(), current_level = 1 WHERE id = $1`,
          [id],
        );
      }
      return { ok: true };
    },
  );

  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/api/admin/events/:id/logs', async (req, reply) => {
    if (!isAdmin(req)) return reply.code(403).send({ error: 'forbidden' });
    const res = await db.query(
      `SELECT * FROM event_logs WHERE event_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100`,
      [req.params.id],
    );
    return { logs: res.rows };
  });
}



========== apps\server\src\game-manager.ts ==========

import { PokerGame } from '../../../packages/poker-engine/src/game.js';
import type { PlayerInit } from '../../../packages/poker-engine/src/types.js';
import { HandPersister } from './hand-persister.js';
import { ErrorCode, type CommandResult } from './errors.js';

export interface PlayerInfo {
  userId: number;
  playerId: string;
  displayName: string;
  socketId: string;
  tournamentStack?: number;
}

export interface TableRoom {
  tableId: number;
  game: PokerGame;
  players: Map<string, PlayerInfo>;
  socketToPlayer: Map<string, string>;
  speed: 'regular' | 'fast' | 'turbo' | 'hyper';
  blinds: { sb: number; bb: number; ante: number };
  variant: 'holdem' | 'omaha4' | 'omaha5';
  mode: 'free' | 'real';
  rakePct: number;
  busy: boolean;
  handTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null;
  turnTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null;
  turnStartedAt: number;
  autoActionAt: number;
  lastActionAt: number;
  handCounter: number;
  totalRebuy: number;
  totalRake: number;
  currentHandId: number | null;
  currentSequence: number;
  tournamentId?: number;
  tournamentTableNumber?: number;
  turnBankUsed: boolean;
  timerGen: number;
  lastScheduledSeat: number;
  scheduledAt: number;
}

const TIME_LIMITS = {
  regular: { action: 30, bank: 30 },
  fast: { action: 15, bank: 15 },
  turbo: { action: 10, bank: 10 },
  hyper: { action: 8, bank: 8 },
};

function round2(n: number): number {
  return Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
}

function ts(): string {
  return new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 23);
}

class GameManager {
  private rooms: Map<number, TableRoom> = new Map();
  private persister: HandPersister | null = null;
  private tournamentHandEndCallback:
    | ((tournamentId: number, room: TableRoom, handResult?: any) => void | Promise<void>)
    | null = null;

  private getPersister(): HandPersister {
    if (!this.persister) {
      const pool = (globalThis as any).__dbPool;
      if (!pool) throw new Error('DB pool not initialized');
      this.persister = new HandPersister(pool);
      console.log('[game-manager] HandPersister initialized');
    }
    return this.persister;
  }

  setTournamentHandEndCallback(
    cb: (tournamentId: number, room: TableRoom, handResult?: any) => void | Promise<void>,
  ): void {
    this.tournamentHandEndCallback = cb;
    console.log('[game-manager] tournament callback registered');
  }

  static getVirtualTableId(tournamentId: number, tableNumber: number): number {
    return 1_000_000 + tournamentId * 1000 + tableNumber;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // TIMER
  // ============================================================
  private clearAutoActionTimer(room: TableRoom): void {
    if (room.turnTimer) {
      clearTimeout(room.turnTimer);
      room.turnTimer = null;
    }
  }

  private getEffectiveLimitSec(room: TableRoom, seat: any): number {
    const playerId = `user_${seat.userId}`;
    const p = room.players.get(playerId);
    if (!p || !p.socketId) return 3;
    const limits = TIME_LIMITS[room.speed];
    return limits.action + (room.turnBankUsed ? limits.bank : 0);
  }

  /**
   * 🔴 Idempotent: if a timer is already running for the SAME seat,
   * DO NOT clear and reschedule (which would reset the clock forever).
   */
  private scheduleAutoAction(room: TableRoom, io: any, reason: string): void {
    if (room.game.state.phase === 'showdown' || room.game.state.phase === 'waiting') {
      return;
    }

    const currentSeat = room.game.state.currentSeat;
    if (currentSeat < 0) return;

    const seat = room.game.state.seats[currentSeat];
    if (!seat || seat.folded || seat.allIn) return;

    // 🔴 If already scheduled for this exact seat, keep the existing timer
    if (room.turnTimer && room.lastScheduledSeat === currentSeat) {
      console.log(
        `[timer] ${ts()} ↺ skip reschedule (seat ${currentSeat} already armed, reason=${reason})`,
      );
      return;
    }

    this.clearAutoActionTimer(room);

    const totalMs = this.getEffectiveLimitSec(room, seat) * 1000;
    const elapsed = Date.now() - room.turnStartedAt;
    const remaining = Math.max(500, totalMs - elapsed);

    room.timerGen += 1;
    const myGen = room.timerGen;
    room.lastScheduledSeat = currentSeat;
    room.scheduledAt = Date.now();

    console.log(
      `[timer] ${ts()} 🕐 gen=${myGen} seat ${currentSeat} (${seat.displayName}): ${Math.round(remaining / 1000)}s reason=${reason}`,
    );

    room.turnTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      const r = this.rooms.get(room.tableId);
      if (!r) return;
      if (r.timerGen !== myGen) {
        console.log(`[timer] ${ts()} ⚠️ stale fired gen=${myGen} (cur=${r.timerGen})`);
        return;
      }
      console.log(`[timer] ${ts()} 🔥 fired gen=${myGen} seat=${r.game.state.currentSeat}`);
      this.autoAction(room.tableId, io).catch((e) =>
        console.error('[timer] auto-action error:', e.message),
      );
    }, remaining);
  }

  private async autoAction(tableId: number, io: any): Promise<void> {
    const room = this.rooms.get(tableId);
    if (!room) return;

    if (room.busy) {
      console.log(`[timer] ${ts()} ⏸️ busy, skip auto-action`);
      return;
    }

    if (room.game.state.phase === 'showdown' || room.game.state.phase === 'waiting') {
      return;
    }

    const currentSeat = room.game.state.currentSeat;
    if (currentSeat < 0) return;

    const seat = room.game.state.seats[currentSeat];
    if (!seat || seat.folded || seat.allIn) return;

    const maxBet = Math.max(...room.game.state.seats.map((s: any) => s.betThisStreet));
    const toCall = maxBet - seat.betThisStreet;
    const action: 'fold' | 'check' = toCall > 0 ? 'fold' : 'check';

    console.log(
      `[timer] ${ts()} ⏰ auto-${action} for seat ${currentSeat} (${seat.displayName}) toCall=${toCall}`,
    );

    room.busy = true;
    try {
      const result = room.game.act(currentSeat, action, 0);
      if (!result.ok) {
        console.warn(`[timer] ${ts()} ❌ auto-action rejected: ${result.error}`);
        room.busy = false;
        return;
      }
      await this.afterAction(room, io, currentSeat, action, 0, 'auto');
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[timer] auto-action failed:', e.message);
      room.busy = false;
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // After any action
  // ============================================================
  private async afterAction(
    room: TableRoom,
    io: any,
    actorSeat: number,
    action: string,
    amount: number,
    source: 'user' | 'auto',
  ): Promise<void> {
    try {
      this.clearAutoActionTimer(room);
      room.lastScheduledSeat = -1; // reset so next schedule always logs

      room.lastActionAt = Date.now();
      room.turnStartedAt = Date.now();
      room.turnBankUsed = false;
      room.game.state.turnBankActive = false;

      const limits = TIME_LIMITS[room.speed];
      room.autoActionAt = room.turnStartedAt + limits.action * 1000;

      // Persist (skip tournaments)
      if (room.currentHandId && !room.tournamentId) {
        room.currentSequence++;
        try {
          this.getPersister().recordEvent(
            room.currentHandId,
            room.currentSequence,
            'ACTION_TAKEN',
            {
              seat: actorSeat,
              userId: room.game.state.seats[actorSeat]?.userId,
              action,
              amount,
              pot: room.game.state.pot,
              phase: room.game.state.phase,
            },
          );
        } catch (e: any) {
          console.error('[game-manager] event persist error:', e.message);
        }
      }

      // Showdown?
      if (room.game.state.phase === 'showdown') {
        await this.handleShowdown(room, io);
        return;
      }

      // Broadcast + schedule next
      this.broadcastState(room.tableId, io);
      this.scheduleAutoAction(room, io, `after-${source}-seat-${actorSeat}`);
    } finally {
      room.busy = false;
    }
  }

  private async handleShowdown(room: TableRoom, io: any): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const handResult = room.game.finishHand();

      if (room.mode === 'real' && !room.tournamentId) {
        room.totalRake = round2(room.totalRake + handResult.rake);
      }

      room.game.state.handNo = room.handCounter;

      const playerStacks = room.game.state.seats.map((s: any) => ({
        seat: s.seat,
        userId: s.userId,
        stack: s.stack,
      }));

      const totalPot =
        handResult.rake + handResult.payouts.reduce((sum: number, p: any) => sum + p.amount, 0);

      if (room.currentHandId && !room.tournamentId) {
        try {
          await this.getPersister().finishHand(
            room.tableId,
            room.currentHandId,
            {
              handId: room.currentHandId,
              pot: totalPot,
              rake: handResult.rake,
              winners: handResult.winners,
              communityCards: room.game.state.community,
              showdownPlayers: handResult.showdownPlayers,
              sidePots: handResult.sidePots || [],
              playerStacks,
            },
            room.mode,
          );
        } catch (e: any) {
          console.error('[game-manager] persister.finishHand error:', e.message);
        }
      }

      for (const [, info] of room.players) {
        try {
          if (!info.socketId) continue;
          io.to(info.socketId).emit('table:hand_result', {
            result: {
              winners: handResult.winners,
              payouts: handResult.payouts,
              rake: handResult.rake,
              revealSeed: handResult.revealSeed,
              showdown: handResult.showdown,
              showdownPlayers: handResult.showdownPlayers,
              sidePots: handResult.sidePots,
              totalRake: room.totalRake,
              totalRebuy: room.totalRebuy,
            },
            state: room.game.publicView(info.userId),
          });
        } catch (e: any) {
          console.error(`[hand_result] ${info.playerId}: ${e.message}`);
        }
      }

      if (room.tournamentId && this.tournamentHandEndCallback) {
        try {
          await this.tournamentHandEndCallback(room.tournamentId, room, handResult);
        } catch (e: any) {
          console.error('[game-manager] tournament callback error:', e.message);
        }
      }

      this.broadcastState(room.tableId, io);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[finishHand] ERROR:', e);
    }

    const handInterval = Number(process.env.HANDS_INTERVAL_MS ?? 5000);
    setTimeout(() => {
      const r = this.rooms.get(room.tableId);
      if (!r) return;
      this.startHand(room.tableId, io).catch((e) => console.error('[hand] error', e));
    }, handInterval);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Room creation
  // ============================================================
  createRoom(params: {
    tableId: number;
    smallBlind: number;
    bigBlind: number;
    ante: number;
    variant: 'holdem' | 'omaha4' | 'omaha5';
    speed: 'regular' | 'fast' | 'turbo' | 'hyper';
    mode: 'free' | 'real';
    rakePct: number;
  }): TableRoom {
    if (this.rooms.has(params.tableId)) {
      return this.rooms.get(params.tableId)!;
    }

    const effectiveRakePct = params.mode === 'free' ? 0 : params.rakePct;

    const engine = new PokerGame(
      {
        tableId: params.tableId,
        variant: params.variant,
        speed: params.speed,
        smallBlind: params.smallBlind,
        bigBlind: params.bigBlind,
        ante: params.ante,
        rakePct: effectiveRakePct,
      },
      [],
    );

    const room: TableRoom = {
      tableId: params.tableId,
      game: engine,
      players: new Map(),
      socketToPlayer: new Map(),
      speed: params.speed,
      blinds: { sb: params.smallBlind, bb: params.bigBlind, ante: params.ante },
      variant: params.variant,
      mode: params.mode,
      rakePct: effectiveRakePct,
      busy: false,
      handTimer: null,
      turnTimer: null,
      turnStartedAt: 0,
      autoActionAt: 0,
      lastActionAt: 0,
      handCounter: 0,
      totalRebuy: 0,
      totalRake: 0,
      currentHandId: null,
      currentSequence: 0,
      turnBankUsed: false,
      timerGen: 0,
      lastScheduledSeat: -1,
      scheduledAt: 0,
    };

    this.rooms.set(params.tableId, room);
    console.log(`[room] created table ${params.tableId} mode=${params.mode}`);
    return room;
  }

  createTournamentRoom(params: {
    tournamentId: number;
    tableNumber: number;
    variant: 'holdem' | 'omaha4' | 'omaha5';
    speed: 'regular' | 'fast' | 'turbo' | 'hyper';
    blinds: { sb: number; bb: number; ante: number };
    players: Array<{ userId: number; displayName: string; stack: number; socketId?: string }>;
  }): TableRoom {
    const virtualTableId = GameManager.getVirtualTableId(params.tournamentId, params.tableNumber);

    if (this.rooms.has(virtualTableId)) {
      return this.rooms.get(virtualTableId)!;
    }

    const room = this.createRoom({
      tableId: virtualTableId,
      smallBlind: params.blinds.sb,
      bigBlind: params.blinds.bb,
      ante: params.blinds.ante,
      variant: params.variant,
      speed: params.speed,
      mode: 'real',
      rakePct: 0,
    });

    room.tournamentId = params.tournamentId;
    room.tournamentTableNumber = params.tableNumber;

    for (const p of params.players) {
      const playerId = `user_${p.userId}`;
      room.players.set(playerId, {
        userId: p.userId,
        playerId,
        displayName: p.displayName,
        socketId: p.socketId || '',
        tournamentStack: p.stack,
      });
    }

    console.log(
      `[room] 🏆 tournament room created: T#${params.tournamentId} table ${params.tableNumber} → virtual ${virtualTableId} (${params.players.length} players)`,
    );
    return room;
  }

  updateRoomBlinds(tournamentId: number, sb: number, bb: number, ante: number): void {
    let count = 0;
    for (const room of this.rooms.values()) {
      if (room.tournamentId === tournamentId) {
        room.blinds = { sb, bb, ante };
        room.game.state.smallBlind = sb;
        room.game.state.bigBlind = bb;
        room.game.state.ante = ante;
        count++;
      }
    }
    if (count > 0) {
      console.log(
        `[room] 🎯 updated blinds T#${tournamentId} (${count} tables): ${sb}/${bb}${ante ? '+' + ante : ''}`,
      );
    }
  }

  getRoom(tableId: number): TableRoom | undefined {
    return this.rooms.get(tableId);
  }

  joinRoom(params: {
    tableId: number;
    userId: number;
    displayName: string;
    socketId: string;
    tableConfig: any;
  }): TableRoom {
    let room = this.rooms.get(params.tableId);
    if (!room) {
      const mode: 'free' | 'real' = params.tableConfig.mode === 'free' ? 'free' : 'real';
      room = this.createRoom({
        tableId: params.tableId,
        smallBlind: Number(params.tableConfig.blinds.sb),
        bigBlind: Number(params.tableConfig.blinds.bb),
        ante: Number(params.tableConfig.blinds.ante ?? 0),
        variant: params.tableConfig.variant,
        speed: params.tableConfig.speed ?? 'regular',
        mode,
        rakePct: Number(params.tableConfig.rake_pct) || 0.03,
      });
    }

    const playerId = `user_${params.userId}`;
    const existing = room.players.get(playerId);

    const info: PlayerInfo = {
      userId: params.userId,
      playerId,
      displayName: params.displayName,
      socketId: params.socketId,
      tournamentStack: existing?.tournamentStack,
    };

    room.players.set(playerId, info);
    room.socketToPlayer.set(params.socketId, playerId);

    console.log(
      `[room] ${params.displayName} joined table ${params.tableId}, size=${room.players.size}${info.tournamentStack ? ' (stack: ' + info.tournamentStack + ')' : ''}`,
    );
    return room;
  }

  leaveRoom(socketId: string): { tableId: number; playerId: string } | null {
    for (const [tableId, room] of this.rooms.entries()) {
      const playerId = room.socketToPlayer.get(socketId);
      if (playerId) {
        room.socketToPlayer.delete(socketId);

        if (room.tournamentId) {
          const p = room.players.get(playerId);
          if (p) p.socketId = '';
          console.log(`[room] ${playerId} disconnected from tournament ${tableId} (kept)`);
        } else {
          room.players.delete(playerId);
          console.log(`[room] ${playerId} left table ${tableId}, size=${room.players.size}`);

          if (room.players.size === 0) {
            this.clearAutoActionTimer(room);
            if (room.handTimer) clearTimeout(room.handTimer);
            this.rooms.delete(tableId);
            console.log(`[room] table ${tableId} destroyed`);
          }
        }
        return { tableId, playerId };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  getPlayerBySocket(socketId: string): { room: TableRoom; player: PlayerInfo } | null {
    for (const room of this.rooms.values()) {
      const playerId = room.socketToPlayer.get(socketId);
      if (playerId) {
        const player = room.players.get(playerId);
        if (player) return { room, player };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  resync(tableId: number, userId: number, lastHandNo: number | null, lastSequence: number | null) {
    const room = this.rooms.get(tableId);
    if (!room) {
      return { syncType: 'full', currentHandNo: 0, currentSequence: 0 };
    }

    const curHand = room.handCounter;
    const curSeq = room.currentSequence;

    if (lastHandNo === curHand && lastSequence === curSeq) {
      return { syncType: 'none', currentHandNo: curHand, currentSequence: curSeq };
    }

    return { syncType: 'full', currentHandNo: curHand, currentSequence: curSeq };
  }

  async startHand(tableId: number, io: any): Promise<void> {
    const room = this.rooms.get(tableId);
    if (!room) return;

    if (room.busy) {
      console.log(`[hand] ${ts()} table ${tableId}: busy, skipping startHand`);
      return;
    }

    const isTournament = !!room.tournamentId;

    const activePlayers = room.players.size;
    if (activePlayers < 2) {
      console.log(`[hand] table ${tableId}: not enough players (${activePlayers})`);
      return;
    }

    room.busy = true;
    try {
      const players: PlayerInit[] = [];
      let reBuysThisHand = 0;
      const REBUY_THRESHOLD = Number(process.env.REBUY_THRESHOLD ?? 0);
      const REBUY_AMOUNT = Number(process.env.REBUY_AMOUNT ?? 1000);

      for (const [, info] of room.players) {
        const oldSeat = room.game.state.seats.find((s) => s.userId === info.userId);
        let stack: number;

        if (isTournament) {
          if (oldSeat === undefined) {
            stack = info.tournamentStack ?? 0;
          } else {
            stack = Number(oldSeat.stack) || 0;
          }
          if (stack <= 0) {
            console.log(`[hand] tournament: skipping user ${info.userId} (stack=${stack})`);
            continue;
          }
        } else {
          stack = oldSeat?.stack ?? 1000;
          if (REBUY_THRESHOLD > 0 && stack < REBUY_THRESHOLD) {
            const injected = round2(REBUY_AMOUNT - stack);
            room.totalRebuy = round2(room.totalRebuy + injected);
            stack = REBUY_AMOUNT;
            reBuysThisHand++;
          }
        }

        players.push({
          userId: info.userId,
          displayName: info.displayName,
          stack,
        });
      }

      if (players.length < 2) {
        console.log(`[hand] table ${tableId}: only ${players.length} players with chips — paused`);
        room.busy = false;
        return;
      }

      const prevHandCounter = room.handCounter ?? 0;
      const prevDealerSeat = room.game.state.dealerSeat;

      room.game = new PokerGame(
        {
          tableId,
          variant: room.variant,
          speed: room.speed,
          smallBlind: room.blinds.sb,
          bigBlind: room.blinds.bb,
          ante: room.blinds.ante,
          rakePct: room.rakePct,
        },
        players,
      );

      if (prevDealerSeat >= 0) {
        room.game.state.dealerSeat = prevDealerSeat;
      }

      room.game.startHand([]);

      room.handCounter = prevHandCounter + 1;
      room.game.state.handNo = room.handCounter;
      room.currentSequence = 0;
      room.turnBankUsed = false;

      room.turnStartedAt = Date.now();
      const limits = TIME_LIMITS[room.speed];
      room.autoActionAt = room.turnStartedAt + limits.action * 1000;

      // Log hand details
      const dealerSeat = room.game.state.dealerSeat;
      const firstSeat = room.game.state.currentSeat;
      console.log(
        `[hand] ${ts()} table ${tableId} hand #${room.handCounter} dealer=${dealerSeat} first=${firstSeat} SB=${room.blinds.sb} BB=${room.blinds.bb}`,
      );

      const handPlayers = room.game.state.seats
        .filter((s: any) => !s.folded || s.stack > 0)
        .map((s: any) => ({
          userId: s.userId,
          displayName: s.displayName,
          stack: s.stack,
          seat: s.seat,
        }));

      // Skip persistence for tournaments
      if (isTournament) {
        room.currentHandId = null;
      } else {
        try {
          const persister = this.getPersister();
          const handId = await persister.startHand({
            tableId,
            handNo: room.handCounter,
            variant: room.variant,
            mode: room.mode,
            smallBlind: room.blinds.sb,
            bigBlind: room.blinds.bb,
            ante: room.blinds.ante,
            dealerSeat: room.game.state.dealerSeat,
            players: handPlayers,
          });
          room.currentHandId = handId;
        } catch (e: any) {
          console.error('[game-manager] persister.startHand error:', e.message);
          room.currentHandId = null;
        }
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(`[hand] ERROR in startHand:`, e);
      room.busy = false;
      return;
    }

    // Release busy
    room.busy = false;

    // Broadcast + schedule first action
    this.broadcastState(tableId, io);
    this.scheduleAutoAction(room, io, `startHand-#${room.handCounter}`);
  }

  async act(socketId: string, actionType: string, amount: number, io: any): Promise<CommandResult> {
    const ctx = this.getPlayerBySocket(socketId);
    if (!ctx) return { ok: false, code: ErrorCode.NOT_IN_ROOM };

    const { room, player } = ctx;

    if (room.busy) {
      return { ok: false, code: ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED };
    }

    const seatIdx = room.game.state.seats.findIndex((s) => s.userId === player.userId);
    if (seatIdx === -1) return { ok: false, code: ErrorCode.NOT_SEATED };

    const actType = actionType.toLowerCase() as 'fold' | 'check' | 'call' | 'raise';
    const result = room.game.act(seatIdx, actType, amount);
    if (!result.ok) {
      return { ok: false, code: (result.error as any) || ErrorCode.INVALID_ACTION };
    }

    console.log(`[hand] ${ts()} user ${player.displayName} seat ${seatIdx} ${actType} ${amount}`);

    room.busy = true;
    await this.afterAction(room, io, seatIdx, actType, amount, 'user');
    return { ok: true };
  }

  async useTimeBank(socketId: string, io: any): Promise<CommandResult> {
    const ctx = this.getPlayerBySocket(socketId);
    if (!ctx) return { ok: false, code: ErrorCode.NOT_IN_ROOM };

    const { room, player } = ctx;
    const seatIdx = room.game.state.seats.findIndex((s) => s.userId === player.userId);
    if (seatIdx === -1) return { ok: false, code: ErrorCode.NOT_SEATED };

    const result = room.game.useTimeBank(seatIdx);
    if (!result.ok) return { ok: false, code: (result.error as any) || ErrorCode.INVALID_ACTION };

    room.turnBankUsed = true;
    room.game.state.turnBankActive = true;
    const limits = TIME_LIMITS[room.speed];
    room.autoActionAt = room.turnStartedAt + (limits.action + limits.bank) * 1000;

    console.log(`[timer] ${ts()} ⏱️ time bank used by seat ${seatIdx} (${player.displayName})`);

    this.clearAutoActionTimer(room);
    room.lastScheduledSeat = -1;
    this.scheduleAutoAction(room, io, 'timeBank');
    this.broadcastState(room.tableId, io);

    return { ok: true };
  }

  broadcastState(tableId: number, io: any): void {
    const room = this.rooms.get(tableId);
    if (!room) return;

    const limits = TIME_LIMITS[room.speed];
    const effectiveLimit = limits.action + (room.turnBankUsed ? limits.bank : 0);

    for (const [, info] of room.players) {
      try {
        if (!info.socketId) continue;
        const view = room.game.publicView(info.userId);
        io.to(info.socketId).emit('table:state', {
          ...view,
          handNo: room.handCounter,
          sequence: room.currentSequence,
          pot: round2(view.pot ?? 0),
          minRaise: round2(view.minRaise ?? 0),
          smallBlind: round2(view.smallBlind ?? 0),
          bigBlind: round2(view.bigBlind ?? 0),
          seats: view.seats.map((s: any) => ({
            ...s,
            stack: round2(s.stack ?? 0),
