      console.error('[scheduler] error:', e.message);
    }
  }

  private async fire(tpl: TemplateRow): Promise<void> {
    try {
      // Create a new tournament from template
      const tid = await this.tm.create({
        name: tpl.name,
        variant: tpl.variant as any,
        mode: tpl.mode as any,
        format: tpl.format as any,
        speed: tpl.speed as any,
        buyIn: Number(tpl.buy_in),
        fee: Number(tpl.fee),
        startingStack: Number(tpl.starting_stack),
        minPlayers: tpl.min_players,
        maxPlayers: tpl.max_players,
        guaranteed: Number(tpl.guaranteed),
        scheduledStart: new Date(),
        lateRegMin: tpl.late_reg_min,
        lateRegLevel: tpl.late_reg_level,
        allowReEntry: tpl.allow_re_entry,
        maxReEntries: tpl.max_re_entries,
        isBounty: tpl.is_bounty,
        bountyAmount: Number(tpl.bounty_amount),
        satelliteTargetId: tpl.satellite_target_id,
        seatsAwarded: tpl.seats_awarded,
        blindStructureId: tpl.blind_structure_id,
        isPrivate: false,
        createdBy: tpl.created_by ?? 1,
      });

      const nextRun = this.computeNextRun(tpl);

      await this.db.query(
        `UPDATE tournament_templates SET last_run_at = NOW(), next_run_at = $1 WHERE id = $2`,
        [nextRun, tpl.id],
      );

      console.log(
        `[scheduler] ✅ fired template #${tpl.id} "${tpl.name}" → tournament #${tid} (next run: ${nextRun.toISOString()})`,
      );
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(`[scheduler] ❌ fire failed for template #${tpl.id}:`, e.message);
      // advance next_run even on failure to avoid stuck loop
      try {
        const nextRun = this.computeNextRun(tpl);
        await this.db.query(
          `UPDATE tournament_templates SET next_run_at = $1 WHERE id = $2`,
          [nextRun, tpl.id],
        );
      } catch {}
    }
  }
}


========== apps\server\src\wallet-routes.ts ==========

import { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { verifyToken } from './auth.js';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.js';
import { tonAdapter } from './ton.js';

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET ?? 'dev_secret_change_me';
const TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS = process.env.TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS || '';
const TON_MIN_WITHDRAW = Number(process.env.TON_MIN_WITHDRAW || 1.0);
const TON_WITHDRAW_FEE = Number(process.env.TON_WITHDRAW_FEE || 0.05);

function authUser(req: any): number | null {
  const auth = req.headers.authorization;
  if (!auth?.startsWith('Bearer ')) return null;
  const payload = verifyToken(auth.slice(7), JWT_SECRET);
  return payload?.uid ?? null;
}

export async function registerWalletRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, db: Pool): Promise<void> {
  const ledger = new LedgerService(db);

  // GET /api/wallet/deposit-info
  app.get('/api/wallet/deposit-info', async (req, reply) => {
    const userId = authUser(req);
    if (!userId) return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'notoken' });

    if (!TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS) {
      return reply.code(503).send({ error: 'deposits_disabled' });
    }

    return {
      depositAddress: TON_DEPOSIT_ADDRESS,
      memo: String(userId),
      minDeposit: tonAdapter.getMinDeposit(),
      network: process.env.TON_NETWORK || 'testnet',
    };
  });

  // GET /api/wallet/balance
  app.get('/api/wallet/balance', async (req, reply) => {
    const userId = authUser(req);
    if (!userId) return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'notoken' });

    try {
      const userAcc = await ledger.getUserAccountId(userId);
      const balance = userAcc ? await ledger.getBalance(userAcc) : 0;

      const ton = await db.query(
        `SELECT
           COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN direction='deposit' AND status='confirmed' THEN amount_ton ELSE 0 END), 0) AS dep,
           COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN direction='withdraw' AND status IN ('confirmed','processing','pending') THEN amount_ton ELSE 0 END), 0) AS wd
         FROM ton_transactions WHERE user_id = $1`,
        [userId],
      );

      const chips = await db.query(`SELECT chips FROM free_chips WHERE user_id = $1`, [userId]);

      return {
        balance: Number(balance),
        freeChips: Number(chips.rows[0]?.chips ?? 0),
        ton: {
          deposited: Number(ton.rows[0].dep),
          withdrawn: Number(ton.rows[0].wd),
        },
        hotWalletReady: tonAdapter.isReady(),
      };
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[wallet] balance failed:', e.message);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: e.message });
    }
  });

  // GET /api/wallet/history
  app.get('/api/wallet/history', async (req, reply) => {
    const userId = authUser(req);
    if (!userId) return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'notoken' });

    try {
      const txs = await db.query(
        `SELECT id, direction, amount_ton, amount_usd, tx_hash, from_address, to_address,
                memo, status, error, confirmations, created_at, confirmed_at
         FROM ton_transactions
         WHERE user_id = $1
         ORDER BY id DESC
         LIMIT 50`,
        [userId],
      );

      const ledgerTxs = await ledger.getHistory(userId, 50);

      return {
        tonTransactions: txs.rows.map((r) => ({
          id: r.id,
          direction: r.direction,
          amountTon: Number(r.amount_ton),
          amountUsd: r.amount_usd ? Number(r.amount_usd) : null,
          txHash: r.tx_hash,
          fromAddress: r.from_address,
          toAddress: r.to_address,
          memo: r.memo,
          status: r.status,
          error: r.error,
          confirmations: r.confirmations,
          createdAt: r.created_at,
          confirmedAt: r.confirmed_at,
        })),
        ledgerTransactions: ledgerTxs,
      };
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[wallet] history failed:', e.message);
      return reply.code(500).send({ error: e.message });
    }
  });

  // POST /api/wallet/withdraw
  app.post<{ Body: { amountTon: number; toAddress: string } }>(
    '/api/wallet/withdraw',
    async (req, reply) => {
      const userId = authUser(req);
      if (!userId) return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'notoken' });

      const { amountTon, toAddress } = req.body ?? {};

      if (!amountTon || amountTon < TON_MIN_WITHDRAW) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'amount_below_minimum', minimum: TON_MIN_WITHDRAW });
      }
      // 🔴 Accept mainnet (EQ/UQ), testnet (kQ/0Q), and raw (0:/-1:)
      const validPrefixes = ['EQ', 'UQ', 'kQ', '0Q', 'Ef', 'Uf', 'kf', '0f', '0:', '-1:'];
      if (!toAddress || !validPrefixes.some((p) => toAddress.startsWith(p))) {
        return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'invalid_address' });
      }
      if (!tonAdapter.isReady()) {
        return reply.code(503).send({ error: 'withdrawals_disabled' });
      }

      try {
        const userAcc = await ledger.getUserAccountId(userId);
        if (!userAcc) return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'no_account' });

        const balance = await ledger.getBalance(userAcc);
        const tonPrice = 5; // TODO: real price feed
        const amountUsd = amountTon * tonPrice;

        if (balance < amountUsd) {
          return reply.code(400).send({
            error: 'insufficient_balance',
            balance,
            required: amountUsd,
          });
        }

        const escrowAcc = await ledger.getAccountId('SYSTEM_ESCROW');
        if (!escrowAcc) return reply.code(500).send({ error: 'escrow_not_found' });

        const ledgerResult = await ledger.record({
          kind: 'WITHDRAWAL',
          userId,
          ref: `withdraw_${userId}_${Date.now()}`,
          meta: { amountTon, toAddress },
          entries: [
            { accountId: userAcc, amount: -amountUsd },
            { accountId: escrowAcc, amount: amountUsd },
          ],
          idempotencyKey: `wd_${userId}_${Date.now()}`,
        });

        if (!ledgerResult.ok) {
          return reply.code(400).send({ error: ledgerResult.error });
        }

        const tonResult = await db.query(
          `INSERT INTO ton_transactions
             (user_id, direction, amount_ton, amount_usd, to_address, status, ledger_tx_id, memo)
           VALUES ($1, 'withdraw', $2, $3, $4, 'pending', $5, $6)
           RETURNING id`,
          [userId, amountTon, amountUsd, toAddress, ledgerResult.transactionId, `wd_${userId}`],
        );

        const txId = tonResult.rows[0].id;

        // Send TON async
        (async () => {
          try {
            await db.query(`UPDATE ton_transactions SET status='processing' WHERE id=$1`, [txId]);
            const txHash = await tonAdapter.sendTon(toAddress, amountTon - TON_WITHDRAW_FEE);
            await db.query(
              `UPDATE ton_transactions SET status='processing', tx_hash=$1 WHERE id=$2`,
              [txHash, txId],
            );
            console.log(`[wallet] withdraw sent: ${txHash}`);
            console.log(`[wallet] waiting for confirmation (checker runs every 15s)...`);
          } catch (e: any) {
            console.error('[wallet] withdraw send failed:', e.message);
            await db.query(`UPDATE ton_transactions SET status='failed', error=$1 WHERE id=$2`, [
              e.message,
              txId,
            ]);
            // Refund
            const escrowAcc2 = await ledger.getAccountId('SYSTEM_ESCROW');
            if (escrowAcc2) {
              await ledger.record({
                kind: 'WITHDRAWAL',
                userId,
                ref: `withdraw_refund_${txId}`,
                entries: [
                  { accountId: escrowAcc2, amount: -amountUsd },
                  { accountId: userAcc, amount: amountUsd },
                ],
                idempotencyKey: `wd_refund_${txId}`,
              });
            }
          }
        })();

        return { ok: true, transactionId: txId, amountTon, toAddress };
      } catch (e: any) {
        console.error('[wallet] withdraw failed:', e.message);
        return reply.code(500).send({ error: e.message });
      }
    },
  );

  // GET /api/wallet/withdraw/:id
  app.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/api/wallet/withdraw/:id', async (req, reply) => {
    const userId = authUser(req);
    if (!userId) return reply.code(401).send({ error: 'notoken' });

    const id = Number(req.params.id);
    if (!id) return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'invalid_id' });

    const res = await db.query(
      `SELECT id, direction, amount_ton, amount_usd, tx_hash, to_address, status, error, created_at, confirmed_at
         FROM ton_transactions WHERE id=$1 AND user_id=$2`,
      [id, userId],
    );
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return reply.code(404).send({ error: 'not_found' });
    return res.rows[0];
  });
}

========== e2e\auth.spec.ts ==========

import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Auth & Navigation', () => {
  test('dev login via ?uid auto-logs in and shows Home', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await expect(page.getByText(/quick seat|instant play|home/i).first()).toBeVisible({
      timeout: 15000,
    });
  });

  test('different users get different sessions', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=222&name=Sara');
    await expect(page.getByText(/quick seat|instant play/i).first()).toBeVisible({
      timeout: 15000,
    });
  });

  test('can navigate to Tournaments tab', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await expect(page.getByText(/quick seat|instant play/i).first()).toBeVisible({
      timeout: 15000,
    });

    const tourneysTab = page.getByText(/tourneys|tournaments/i).first();
    if (await tourneysTab.isVisible()) {
      await tourneysTab.click();
      await expect(page.getByText(/tournaments|no tournaments/i).first()).toBeVisible({
        timeout: 5000,
      });
    }
  });

  test('can navigate to Tables tab', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await expect(page.getByText(/quick seat|instant play/i).first()).toBeVisible({
      timeout: 15000,
    });

    const tablesTab = page.getByText(/^tables$/i).first();
    if (await tablesTab.isVisible()) {
      await tablesTab.click();
      await expect(page.getByText(/tables|no tables|blinds/i).first()).toBeVisible({
        timeout: 5000,
      });
    }
  });

  test('splash screen appears during initial load', async ({ page }) => {
    const navPromise = page.goto('/?uid=333&name=TestUser');
    await navPromise;
    await expect(page.getByText(/quick seat|instant play/i).first()).toBeVisible({
      timeout: 15000,
    });
  });
});



========== e2e\tournaments.spec.ts ==========

import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Tournaments', () => {
  test('can open Tournaments tab and see list', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await expect(page.getByText(/quick seat|instant play/i).first()).toBeVisible({
      timeout: 15000,
    });

    const tab = page.getByText(/tourneys|tournaments/i).first();
    await tab.click();

    await expect(page.locator('h1:has-text("Tournaments")').first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 5000 });
  });

  test('shows tournament list or empty state', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await page.waitForSelector('text=/quick seat|instant play/i', { timeout: 15000 });

    const tab = page.getByText(/tourneys|tournaments/i).first();
    await tab.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(1500);

    const hasCards = await page.locator('[class*="rounded-2xl"] button').count();
    const noTourneyText = await page.getByText(/no tournaments/i).count();
    const headerVisible = await page.locator('h1:has-text("Tournaments")').count();

    expect(headerVisible).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(hasCards > 0 || noTourneyText > 0 || headerVisible > 0).toBe(true);
  });

  test('filter buttons are clickable', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await page.waitForSelector('text=/quick seat|instant play/i', { timeout: 15000 });

    const tab = page.getByText(/tourneys|tournaments/i).first();
    await tab.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(1000);

    const allFilter = page.getByText(/^all$/i).first();
    if (await allFilter.isVisible().catch(() => false)) {
      await allFilter.click();
      await page.waitForTimeout(500);
    }

    expect(await page.locator('h1:has-text("Tournaments")').count()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});



========== e2e\wallet.spec.ts ==========

import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('Wallet', () => {
  test('can navigate to Wallet page', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await expect(page.getByText(/quick seat|instant play/i).first()).toBeVisible({
      timeout: 15000,
    });

    // کلیک روی تب Wallet
    const walletTab = page.getByText(/^wallet$/i).first();
    await walletTab.click();

    // صفحه Wallet باید بیاد
    await expect(page.locator('h1:has-text("Wallet")').first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 5000 });
  });

  test('shows balance and free chips', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await page.waitForSelector('text=/quick seat|instant play/i', { timeout: 15000 });

    const walletTab = page.getByText(/^wallet$/i).first();
    await walletTab.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(1000);

    // Balance card باید نمایش داده بشه
    const balanceText = await page.getByText(/real money balance/i).count();
    const freeChipsText = await page.getByText(/free chips/i).count();

    expect(balanceText + freeChipsText).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('has Deposit, Withdraw, History tabs', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await page.waitForSelector('text=/quick seat|instant play/i', { timeout: 15000 });

    const walletTab = page.getByText(/^wallet$/i).first();
    await walletTab.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(1000);

    // ۳ تب باید باشن
    const deposit = page.getByText(/^deposit$/i).first();
    const withdraw = page.getByText(/^withdraw$/i).first();
    const history = page.getByText(/^history$/i).first();

    expect(await deposit.count()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(await withdraw.count()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(await history.count()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('can switch between Deposit / Withdraw / History tabs', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await page.waitForSelector('text=/quick seat|instant play/i', { timeout: 15000 });

    const walletTab = page.getByText(/^wallet$/i).first();
    await walletTab.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(1000);

    // برو به Withdraw
    const withdraw = page.getByText(/^withdraw$/i).first();
    await withdraw.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(500);

    // باید "To Address" یا "Amount" ببینیم
    const hasWithdrawContent = await page.getByText(/to address|amount/i).count();
    expect(hasWithdrawContent).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // برو به History
    const history = page.getByText(/^history$/i).first();
    await history.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(500);

    // باید "No transactions yet" یا لیست ببینیم
    const hasHistoryContent = await page.getByText(/no transactions|deposit|withdraw/i).count();
    expect(hasHistoryContent).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('deposit tab shows address info or disabled message', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/?uid=111&name=Ali');
    await page.waitForSelector('text=/quick seat|instant play/i', { timeout: 15000 });

    const walletTab = page.getByText(/^wallet$/i).first();
    await walletTab.click();
    await page.waitForTimeout(1500);

    // Deposit tab پیش‌فرض بازه — یا آدرس نشون می‌ده یا پیام disabled
    const hasAddress = await page.getByText(/deposit address|EQ|0Q|kQ/i).count();
    const hasDisabled = await page.getByText(/deposits are currently disabled/i).count();
    const hasNetwork = await page.getByText(/testnet|mainnet/i).count();

    expect(hasAddress + hasDisabled + hasNetwork).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});



========== infra\sql\001_init.sql ==========

 CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

CREATE TABLE users (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  telegram_id     BIGINT UNIQUE,
  username        TEXT,
  display_name    TEXT,
  language        TEXT DEFAULT 'en',
  country         TEXT,
  is_banned       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  risk_score      INT DEFAULT 0,
  avatar_id       TEXT DEFAULT 'spade',
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE wallets (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id         BIGINT UNIQUE REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  balance_usd     NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0 CHECK (balance_usd >= 0),
  locked_usd      NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE accounts (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  code            TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  type            TEXT NOT NULL,
  owner_user_id   BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE transactions (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  kind            TEXT NOT NULL,
  ref             TEXT,
  meta            JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE entries (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  tx_id           BIGINT REFERENCES transactions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  account_id      BIGINT REFERENCES accounts(id),
  amount          NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX idx_entries_tx ON entries(tx_id);

CREATE TABLE poker_tables (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name            TEXT NOT NULL,
  variant         TEXT NOT NULL,
  mode            TEXT NOT NULL,
  speed           TEXT DEFAULT 'regular',
  blinds          JSONB NOT NULL,
  min_buyin       NUMERIC(18,2),
  max_buyin       NUMERIC(18,2),
  rake_pct        NUMERIC(5,4) DEFAULT 0.03,
  seats           INT DEFAULT 6,
  is_active       BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE hands (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  table_id        BIGINT REFERENCES poker_tables(id),
  hand_no         BIGINT,
  server_seed_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  server_seed     TEXT,
  client_seeds    JSONB,
  deck_hash       TEXT,
  pot             NUMERIC(18,2),
  rake            NUMERIC(18,2),
  result          JSONB,
  started_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  ended_at        TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE TABLE exchange_rates (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  asset           TEXT NOT NULL,
  rate_usd        NUMERIC(18,8) NOT NULL,
  spread_pct      NUMERIC(5,4) DEFAULT 0.025,
  source          TEXT,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE leaderboard_periods (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  period_start    DATE NOT NULL,
  period_end      DATE NOT NULL,
  prize_pool_usd  NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL,
  status          TEXT DEFAULT 'open'
);

CREATE TABLE leaderboard_entries (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  period_id       BIGINT REFERENCES leaderboard_periods(id),
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  hands_played    INT DEFAULT 0,
  total_won_usd   NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  rank            INT,
  prize_usd       NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  paid            BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
);
CREATE INDEX idx_lb_period ON leaderboard_entries(period_id, rank);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uniq_lb_period_user ON leaderboard_entries(period_id, user_id);

CREATE TABLE events (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  title           TEXT NOT NULL,
  variant         TEXT NOT NULL,
  starts_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  buyin_usd       NUMERIC(18,2),
  prize_pool_usd  NUMERIC(18,2),
  max_players     INT,
  status          TEXT DEFAULT 'scheduled',
  structure       JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE event_registrations (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  event_id        BIGINT REFERENCES events(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  registered_at   TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(event_id, user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE revenue_daily (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  date            DATE UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  rake_total      NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  referral_paid   NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  prize_paid      NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  deposit_fees    NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  withdraw_fees   NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  infra_cost      NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  net_revenue     NUMERIC(18,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    rake_total - referral_paid - prize_paid - deposit_fees - withdraw_fees - infra_cost
  ) STORED,
  prize_ratio     NUMERIC(6,4) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    CASE WHEN rake_total > 0 THEN prize_paid / rake_total ELSE 0 END
  ) STORED,
  status          TEXT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
    CASE
      WHEN rake_total = 0 THEN 'idle'
      WHEN prize_paid / NULLIF(rake_total,0) < 0.10 THEN 'ok'
      WHEN prize_paid / NULLIF(rake_total,0) < 0.15 THEN 'warning'
      ELSE 'critical'
    END
  ) STORED,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE alerts (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  type            TEXT NOT NULL,
  message         TEXT NOT NULL,
  resolved        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE free_chips (
  user_id         BIGINT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  chips           BIGINT DEFAULT 10000,
  last_refill_at  TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  total_hands     INT DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE TABLE referrals (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  referrer_id     BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  referee_id      BIGINT UNIQUE REFERENCES users(id),
  earned_usd      NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

INSERT INTO poker_tables (name, variant, mode, speed, blinds, min_buyin, max_buyin, rake_pct, seats) VALUES
  ('Free Beginner', 'holdem', 'free', 'regular', '{"sb":5,"bb":10,"ante":0}',      0, 0,    0,    6),
  ('Free Omaha4',   'omaha4', 'free', 'regular', '{"sb":5,"bb":10,"ante":0}',      0, 0,    0,    6),
  ('Micro Holdem',  'holdem', 'real', 'regular', '{"sb":0.05,"bb":0.10,"ante":0}', 5, 20,   0.03, 6),
  ('Low Holdem',    'holdem', 'real', 'regular', '{"sb":0.25,"bb":0.50,"ante":0}', 20, 100, 0.03, 6),
  ('Mid Omaha4',    'omaha4', 'real', 'regular', '{"sb":0.50,"bb":1.00,"ante":0}', 40, 200, 0.03, 6),
  ('High Holdem',   'holdem', 'real', 'regular', '{"sb":2.50,"bb":5.00,"ante":0}', 200, 1000, 0.03, 6);


========== infra\sql\002_anticheat.sql ==========

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS player_actions (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  table_id        BIGINT,
  hand_no         BIGINT,
  action          TEXT NOT NULL,
  amount          NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  street          TEXT,
  response_ms     INT,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_actions_user ON player_actions(user_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_actions_table_hand ON player_actions(table_id, hand_no);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_ips (
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  ip              TEXT NOT NULL,
  first_seen      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  last_seen       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, ip)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_ips_ip ON user_ips(ip);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_devices (
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  device_hash     TEXT NOT NULL,
  first_seen      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  last_seen       TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, device_hash)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_devices_hash ON user_devices(device_hash);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS player_pairs (
  user_a              BIGINT NOT NULL,
  user_b              BIGINT NOT NULL,
  hands_together      INT DEFAULT 0,
  chips_net_flow      NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  suspicious_moves    INT DEFAULT 0,
  suspicion_score     INT DEFAULT 0,
  last_updated        TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (user_a, user_b),
  CHECK (user_a < user_b)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS suspicious_events (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  other_user_id   BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  type            TEXT NOT NULL,
  severity        INT DEFAULT 1,
  evidence        JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
  resolved        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  reviewer_note   TEXT,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_suspicious_user ON suspicious_events(user_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_suspicious_type ON suspicious_events(type, resolved);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS player_risk (
  user_id         BIGINT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  score           INT DEFAULT 0,
  flags           JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
  action_taken    TEXT DEFAULT 'none',
  last_updated    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

========== infra\sql\003_leaderboard_cron.sql ==========

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_leaderboard_entry(
  p_user_id BIGINT,
  p_won NUMERIC,
  p_hand_no BIGINT
) RETURNS VOID AS $$
DECLARE
  v_period_id BIGINT;
BEGIN
  SELECT id INTO v_period_id
  FROM leaderboard_periods
  WHERE status = 'open' AND NOW() BETWEEN period_start AND period_end
  ORDER BY period_start DESC LIMIT 1;

  IF v_period_id IS NULL THEN
    INSERT INTO leaderboard_periods (period_start, period_end, prize_pool_usd, status)
    VALUES (
      date_trunc('month', NOW())::date,
      (date_trunc('month', NOW()) + INTERVAL '1 month - 1 day')::date,
      0,
      'open'
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_period_id;
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO leaderboard_entries (period_id, user_id, hands_played, total_won_usd)
  VALUES (v_period_id, p_user_id, 1, p_won)
  ON CONFLICT (period_id, user_id) DO UPDATE
  SET hands_played = leaderboard_entries.hands_played + 1,
      total_won_usd = leaderboard_entries.total_won_usd + p_won;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_lb_entries_won
  ON leaderboard_entries(period_id, total_won_usd DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_lb_entries_user_period
  ON leaderboard_entries(user_id, period_id);

ALTER TABLE events
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS blind_structure JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS prize_structure JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS min_players INT DEFAULT 2,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS registered_count INT DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS starting_stack INT DEFAULT 1500,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS late_reg_level INT DEFAULT 3,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS prize_pool_collected NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS current_level INT DEFAULT 0,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS level_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_by BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS finished_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cancelled_reason TEXT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_results (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  event_id        BIGINT REFERENCES events(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id         BIGINT REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  rank            INT NOT NULL,
  prize_usd       NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  hands_played    INT DEFAULT 0,
  eliminated_at   TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(event_id, user_id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournament_results_event ON tournament_results(event_id, rank);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_logs (
  id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  event_id        BIGINT REFERENCES events(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type            TEXT NOT NULL,
  message         TEXT,
  meta            JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_event_logs_event ON event_logs(event_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_status_start ON events(status, starts_at);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION default_blind_structure(p_starting_stack INT DEFAULT 1500)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN '[
    {"level":1,"sb":10,"bb":20,"ante":0,"duration_min":5},
    {"level":2,"sb":15,"bb":30,"ante":0,"duration_min":5},
    {"level":3,"sb":25,"bb":50,"ante":0,"duration_min":5},
    {"level":4,"sb":50,"bb":100,"ante":10,"duration_min":6},
    {"level":5,"sb":75,"bb":150,"ante":15,"duration_min":6},
    {"level":6,"sb":100,"bb":200,"ante":25,"duration_min":8},
    {"level":7,"sb":150,"bb":300,"ante":40,"duration_min":8},
    {"level":8,"sb":200,"bb":400,"ante":50,"duration_min":10},
    {"level":9,"sb":300,"bb":600,"ante":75,"duration_min":10},
    {"level":10,"sb":500,"bb":1000,"ante":100,"duration_min":12},
    {"level":11,"sb":800,"bb":1600,"ante":200,"duration_min":12},
    {"level":12,"sb":1200,"bb":2400,"ante":300,"duration_min":15}
  ]'::jsonb;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION default_prize_structure()
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN '[
    {"rank":1,"pct":0.40},
    {"rank":2,"pct":0.24},
    {"rank":3,"pct":0.15},
    {"rank":4,"pct":0.10},
    {"rank":5,"pct":0.06},
    {"rank":"6-10","pct":0.01}
  ]'::jsonb;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

INSERT INTO events (title, variant, starts_at, buyin_usd, prize_pool_usd, max_players, status, blind_structure, prize_structure, starting_stack)
SELECT 'Daily Freeroll #1', 'holdem', NOW() + INTERVAL '1 hour', 0, 100, 100, 'scheduled',
  default_blind_structure(), default_prize_structure(), 1500
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM events WHERE title = 'Daily Freeroll #1');

INSERT INTO events (title, variant, starts_at, buyin_usd, prize_pool_usd, max_players, status, blind_structure, prize_structure, starting_stack)
SELECT 'Weekly Championship', 'holdem', NOW() + INTERVAL '1 day', 5, 500, 200, 'scheduled',
  default_blind_structure(), default_prize_structure(), 2000
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM events WHERE title = 'Weekly Championship');

========== infra\sql\006_ledger_and_events.sql ==========

 -- Hand events for replay
CREATE TABLE hand_events (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  hand_id BIGINT,
  sequence INT NOT NULL,
  event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  payload JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Snapshots every 10 events
CREATE TABLE hand_snapshots (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  hand_id BIGINT,
  sequence INT NOT NULL,
  state JSONB NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Idempotency keys
CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
  key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT,
  result JSONB,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Table seats
CREATE TABLE table_seats (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  table_id BIGINT,
  seat_index INT NOT NULL,
  user_id BIGINT,
  stack NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'EMPTY',
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Play money log
CREATE TABLE play_money_log (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT,
  delta BIGINT NOT NULL,
  reason TEXT NOT NULL,
  balance_after BIGINT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- System accounts
INSERT INTO accounts (code, type) VALUES
  ('HOUSE_RAKE', 'house'),
  ('HOUSE_PRIZE_POOL', 'house'),
  ('HOUSE_REFERRAL', 'house'),
  ('HOUSE_BONUS', 'house'),
  ('SYSTEM_ESCROW', 'system'),
  ('SYSTEM_DEPOSITS', 'system'),
  ('SYSTEM_WITHDRAWS', 'system')
ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;

-- Auto account function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_user_account() RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO accounts (code, type, owner_user_id)
  VALUES ('USER_' || NEW.id, 'user', NEW.id)
  ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Trigger on users
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_create_user_account ON users;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_create_user_account
AFTER INSERT ON users
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION create_user_account();

-- Backfill existing users
INSERT INTO accounts (code, type, owner_user_id)
SELECT 'USER_' || id, 'user', id FROM users
ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;


========== infra\sql\007_extend_ledger.sql ==========

-- Extend transactions
ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS idempotency_key TEXT;
ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS status TEXT DEFAULT 'completed';
ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS user_id BIGINT REFERENCES users(id);
ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS currency TEXT DEFAULT 'USD';
ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS amount NUMERIC(18,2);
ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW();

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_transactions_user ON transactions(user_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_transactions_kind ON transactions(kind, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_transactions_idem ON transactions(idempotency_key);

-- Extend entries
ALTER TABLE entries ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS currency TEXT DEFAULT 'USD';
ALTER TABLE entries ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS balance_after NUMERIC(18,2);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_entries_account ON entries(account_id, created_at DESC);

-- Extend hands
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS variant TEXT;
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS dealer_seat INT;
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS community_cards JSONB;
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS winner_seats JSONB;
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS engine_version TEXT DEFAULT 'poker-engine@0.3.0';
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS small_blind NUMERIC(18,2);
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS big_blind NUMERIC(18,2);
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ante NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS player_count INT;
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS finished BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE hands ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS mode TEXT;

========== infra\sql\009_fix_events_unique.sql ==========

-- Add FK and unique constraints to hand_events
ALTER TABLE hand_events 
  ADD CONSTRAINT hand_events_hand_fk 
  FOREIGN KEY (hand_id) REFERENCES hands(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;

ALTER TABLE hand_events 
  ADD CONSTRAINT hand_events_hand_seq_unique 
  UNIQUE (hand_id, sequence);

-- Add FK and unique constraints to hand_snapshots
ALTER TABLE hand_snapshots 
  ADD CONSTRAINT hand_snapshots_hand_fk 
  FOREIGN KEY (hand_id) REFERENCES hands(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;

ALTER TABLE hand_snapshots 
  ADD CONSTRAINT hand_snapshots_hand_seq_unique 
  UNIQUE (hand_id, sequence);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hand_events_hand ON hand_events(hand_id, sequence);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hand_snapshots_hand ON hand_snapshots(hand_id, sequence);


========== infra\sql\010_fix_idempotency.sql ==========

ALTER TABLE idempotency_keys ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS scope TEXT DEFAULT 'default';
ALTER TABLE idempotency_keys ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_idempotency_expires ON idempotency_keys(expires_at);

  amount_ton NUMERIC(18,9) NOT NULL,

  to_address TEXT,

  error TEXT,

);

========== infra\sql\012_tournaments.sql ==========

-- ============================================================
-- Blind Structures (templates)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blind_structures (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  description TEXT,
  starting_stack BIGINT NOT NULL,
  is_turbo BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  is_hyper BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blind_levels (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  structure_id BIGINT REFERENCES blind_structures(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  level INT NOT NULL,
  small_blind NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL,
  big_blind NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL,
  ante NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  duration_sec INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 600,
  is_break BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  UNIQUE(structure_id, level)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_blind_levels_struct ON blind_levels(structure_id, level);

-- ============================================================
-- Tournaments
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournaments (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  variant TEXT NOT NULL,
  mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'real',
  format TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'mtt',
  speed TEXT DEFAULT 'regular',
  buy_in NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  fee NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  starting_stack BIGINT NOT NULL,
  min_players INT DEFAULT 2,
  max_players INT DEFAULT 1000,
  guaranteed NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  scheduled_start TIMESTAMPTZ,
  late_reg_min INT DEFAULT 0,
  blind_structure_id BIGINT REFERENCES blind_structures(id),
  current_level INT DEFAULT 0,
  level_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'scheduled',
  -- status: scheduled | registering | running | paused | final_table | finished | cancelled
  is_private BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  invite_code TEXT,
  password_hash TEXT,
  created_by BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  finished_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  winner_user_id BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  total_prize_pool NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  total_rake NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  total_players INT DEFAULT 0,
  config JSONB DEFAULT '{}'
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_status ON tournaments(status, scheduled_start);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tournaments_variant ON tournaments(variant, format);

-- ============================================================
-- Tournament Entries
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_entries (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  tournament_id BIGINT REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  registered_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'registered',
  -- status: registered | playing | eliminated | won | cashed | refunded
  starting_stack BIGINT,
  final_position INT,
  payout_amount NUMERIC(18,2) DEFAULT 0,
  rebuy_count INT DEFAULT 0,
  UNIQUE(tournament_id, user_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_t_entries_tourney ON tournament_entries(tournament_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_t_entries_user ON tournament_entries(user_id, registered_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- Tournament Players (active in-play state)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_players (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  tournament_id BIGINT REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  stack BIGINT NOT NULL,
  table_number INT,
  seat INT,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'active',
  -- status: active | sitting_out | eliminated | winner
  eliminated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  eliminated_by BIGINT REFERENCES users(id),
  final_position INT,
  UNIQUE(tournament_id, user_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_t_players_tourney ON tournament_players(tournament_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_t_players_table ON tournament_players(tournament_id, table_number, seat);

-- ============================================================
-- Tournament Tables
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_tables (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  tournament_id BIGINT REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  table_number INT NOT NULL,
  max_seats INT DEFAULT 9,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'active',
  is_final BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(tournament_id, table_number)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_t_tables_tourney ON tournament_tables(tournament_id, status);

-- ============================================================
-- Tournament Payouts (structure per tournament)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_payouts (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  tournament_id BIGINT REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  position INT NOT NULL,
  percentage NUMERIC(6,4) NOT NULL,
  fixed_amount NUMERIC(18,2),
  UNIQUE(tournament_id, position)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_t_payouts_tourney ON tournament_payouts(tournament_id, position);

-- ============================================================
-- Tournament Blind Level Log (history)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tournament_level_log (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  tournament_id BIGINT REFERENCES tournaments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  level INT NOT NULL,
  sb NUMERIC(18,2),
  bb NUMERIC(18,2),
  ante NUMERIC(18,2),
  players_remaining INT,
  started_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  UNIQUE(tournament_id, level)
);

-- ============================================================
-- Wallet ledger accounts for tournaments
-- ============================================================
INSERT INTO accounts (code, type) VALUES
  ('SYSTEM_TOURNAMENT_ENTRY', 'system'),
  ('SYSTEM_TOURNAMENT_PRIZE', 'system'),
  ('SYSTEM_TOURNAMENT_RAKE', 'system'),
  ('SYSTEM_TOURNAMENT_REFUND', 'system')
ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;
